--- src\components\ShowroomStage.astro ---

import { commissions, materialStudies, showroom, site } from '../data/site';

interface Props {
  mode: 'fullscreen' | 'inline';
}

const { mode } = Astro.props;
const isInline = mode === 'inline';
const pinWaypoints = showroom.waypoints.filter((w) => w.pin);
const studyByKey = new Map(materialStudies.map((m) => [m.key, m]));
const leadStudy = materialStudies[0];
const trackViewports = isInline ? 6 : showroom.trackViewports;
---

<section
  id={isInline ? 'showroom-inline' : undefined}
  class={isInline ? 'showroom-root showroom-inline-track' : 'showroom-root'}
  data-showroom-root
  data-showroom-mode={mode}
  style={`--track: ${trackViewports};`}
  aria-label="Material Study — 3D concept preview of material palette"
>
  {!isInline && (
    <header class="showroom-chrome">
      <div class="showroom-chrome__left">
        <a class="showroom-brand" href="/" aria-label={`${site.name} Home`}>
          <svg width="20" height="24" viewBox="0 0 120 140" fill="none" aria-hidden="true">
            <path
              d="M36 16H84L104 92C89 83 75 78 60 78C45 78 31 83 16 92L36 16ZM60 40L44 74C50 72 55 71 60 71C65 71 70 72 76 74L60 40Z"
              fill="currentColor"
            />
            <path
              d="M35 97C46 105 74 105 85 97C86 112 75 119 66 123C62 125 60 130 61 135C52 130 48 122 50 116C52 112 46 109 42 114C37 110 34 103 35 97Z"
              fill="currentColor"
            />
          </svg>
          <span>{site.name}</span>
        </a>
        <span class="showroom-label">{showroom.label ?? 'Material Study'}</span>
      </div>

      <div class="showroom-chrome__right">
        <button
          class="showroom-sound"
          type="button"
          data-showroom-sound
          aria-pressed="false"
          aria-label="Toggle architectural room sound"
        >
          <span class="sound-bars" aria-hidden="true"><i></i><i></i><i></i><i></i></span>
          <span class="sr-only" data-sound-label>Sound off</span>
        </button>

        <a class="showroom-exit" href="/" data-showroom-exit aria-label="Exit Material Study and return home">
          <span aria-hidden="true">&larr;</span>
          <span>Exit</span>
        </a>
      </div>
    </header>
  )}

  <div class={isInline ? 'showroom-inline-sticky' : 'showroom-fullscreen-shell'}>
    {isInline && (
      <div class="showroom-inline-bar">
        <span class="showroom-inline-kicker">Material Study &middot; Five Connected Spaces</span>
        <a class="showroom-inline-fs" href="/showroom/" data-enter-showroom>
          <span>Fullscreen Walk</span>
          <span aria-hidden="true">&#8599;</span>
        </a>
      </div>
    )}

    <div class="showroom-status" data-showroom-status role="status" aria-live="polite">
      Preparing material study &middot; 0%
    </div>

    <div class="showroom-banner" data-room-banner aria-live="polite">
      <span class="showroom-banner__index" data-room-banner-index>{showroom.waypoints[0].index}</span>
      <p class="showroom-banner__title" data-room-banner-title>{showroom.waypoints[0].label}</p>
    </div>

    <div class="showroom-stage" aria-label="3D material study viewport">
      <div class="showroom-canvas-host" data-showroom-canvas></div>
      <div class="showroom-atmosphere" aria-hidden="true">
        <div class="showroom-atmosphere__vignette"></div>
        <div class="showroom-atmosphere__grain"></div>
      </div>
      <div class="showroom-scrim-top" aria-hidden="true"></div>
      <div class="showroom-scrim-bottom" aria-hidden="true"></div>
    </div>

    <div class="showroom-pins" aria-label="Material palette markers">
      {pinWaypoints.map((wp) => {
        return (
          <div class="showroom-pin" data-showroom-pin={wp.id} aria-hidden="true">
            <button
              type="button"
              class="showroom-pin__btn"
              tabindex="-1"
              aria-label={`Inspect material: ${wp.label}`}
            >
              <span class="showroom-pin__ring" aria-hidden="true">
                <span class="showroom-pin__dot"></span>
              </span>
              <span class="showroom-pin__leader" aria-hidden="true"></span>
              <span class="showroom-pin__chip">
                <span>{wp.index}</span>
                <span>{wp.label}</span>
              </span>
            </button>
          </div>
        );
      })}
    </div>

    <footer class="showroom-footer">
      <p class="showroom-caption">Concept preview of our material palette — not a specific completed project.</p>
      <p class="showroom-hint" data-showroom-hint>Scroll to walk &middot; Drag to look</p>

      <nav class="showroom-chapters-wrap" aria-label="Material study chapters">
        <div class="showroom-rail-track" aria-hidden="true">
          <div class="showroom-rail-fill" data-chapter-fill></div>
        </div>
        <div class="showroom-chapters">
          {showroom.waypoints.map((wp, idx) => (
            <button
              type="button"
              class={`showroom-chapter ${idx === 0 ? 'is-active' : ''}`}
              data-chapter-btn={wp.id}
              data-chapter-u={wp.u}
              aria-current={idx === 0 ? 'step' : undefined}
            >
              <span class="showroom-chapter__num">{wp.index}</span>
              <span class="showroom-chapter__label">{wp.label}</span>
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      </nav>
    </footer>
  </div>

  {!isInline && <div class="showroom-spacer" aria-hidden="true"></div>}

  <aside
    class="showroom-drawer"
    data-showroom-drawer
    role="dialog"
    aria-modal="true"
    aria-labelledby={isInline ? 'sdrawer-title-inline' : 'sdrawer-title'}
    hidden
  >
    <div class="showroom-drawer__backdrop" data-sdrawer-backdrop></div>
    <div class="showroom-drawer__panel">
      <div class="showroom-drawer__top">
        <span class="showroom-drawer__code" data-sdrawer-code>{leadStudy.category}</span>
        <button type="button" class="showroom-drawer__close" data-sdrawer-close aria-label="Close material drawer">
          Close
        </button>
      </div>

      <div>
        <p class="showroom-drawer__disc" data-sdrawer-disc>Concept Preview &middot; Material Palette</p>
        <h2 id={isInline ? 'sdrawer-title-inline' : 'sdrawer-title'} class="showroom-drawer__title" data-sdrawer-title>
          {leadStudy.title}
        </h2>
      </div>

      <figure class="showroom-drawer__media">
        <img data-sdrawer-img src={leadStudy.image.src} alt={leadStudy.alt} loading="lazy" />
      </figure>

      <p class="showroom-drawer__summary" data-sdrawer-summary>{leadStudy.summary}</p>

      <div>
        <p class="showroom-drawer__section-label">Material Specification</p>
        <ul class="showroom-drawer__materials" data-sdrawer-materials></ul>
      </div>

      <div class="showroom-drawer__actions">
        <a class="showroom-drawer__cta" data-sdrawer-study href="/works/">
          <span>Explore Completed Works</span>
          <span aria-hidden="true">&#8599;</span>
        </a>
        <a
          class="showroom-drawer__inquire"
          data-sdrawer-inquire
          href={`https://wa.me/${site.whatsappNumber}`}
          target="_blank"
          rel="noopener noreferrer"
        >
          <span>Inquire about this finish</span>
          <span aria-hidden="true">&#8599;</span>
        </a>
      </div>
    </div>
  </aside>

  <div class="showroom-fallback" aria-label="Material study directory">
    <div class="showroom-fallback__inner">
      <div class="showroom-fallback__head">
        <span class="showroom-drawer__code">Material Study</span>
        <h2 class="showroom-fallback__title">Five material concepts in Eastern Nepal daylight.</h2>
        <p class="showroom-drawer__summary">
          Concept preview of our material palette — not a specific completed project. Honed Kota limestone, fluted dark Sal timber, deep Verde Alpi stone, and quiet mineral finishes across Biratnagar and Dharan.
        </p>
      </div>

      <div class="showroom-fallback__grid">
        {commissions.map((c) => (
          <article class="showroom-fallback__card">
            <div class="showroom-fallback__imgwrap">
              <img src={c.image.src} alt={c.alt} loading="eager" />
            </div>
            <div class="showroom-fallback__cardbody">
              <span class="showroom-drawer__code">{c.location} &middot; {c.typology} &middot; {c.year}</span>
              <h3 class="showroom-fallback__cardtitle">{c.title}</h3>
              <p class="showroom-drawer__summary">{c.summary}</p>
              <a class="showroom-fallback__link" href={`/works/${c.slug}/`}>
                View room dossier &#8599;
              </a>
            </div>
          </article>
        ))}
      </div>

      <ol class="showroom-fallback__list">
        {showroom.waypoints.map((wp) => {
          const study = wp.pin ? studyByKey.get(wp.pin.studySlug) : undefined;
          return (
            <li class="showroom-fallback__item">
              <span>
                <strong>{wp.index}</strong> &middot; {wp.label}
              </span>
              {study ? (
                <a class="showroom-fallback__link" href="/works/">
                  {study.category} &middot; Works &#8599;
                </a>
              ) : (
                <span class="showroom-drawer__disc">Material Palette Threshold</span>
              )}
            </li>
          );
        })}
      </ol>
    </div>
  </div>

  <noscript>
    <style>
      .showroom-stage, .showroom-pins, .showroom-footer, .showroom-status, .showroom-spacer, .showroom-inline-bar { display: none !important; }
      .showroom-fallback { display: block !important; }
    </style>
  </noscript>
</section>

import realDiningHall from '../assets/real-dining-hall.jpg';
import realLivingRoom from '../assets/real-living-room.jpg';
import realMasterBedroom from '../assets/real-master-bedroom.jpg';
import realReadingCorner from '../assets/real-reading-corner.jpg';
import realLoungeVignette from '../assets/real-lounge-vignette.jpg';
import rubiconiusPracticeStudio from '../assets/rubiconius-practice-studio.jpg';
import rubiconiusHeroAi from '../assets/rubiconius-hero-ai.jpg';

export type GlbPinKey = 'fluted-suite' | 'timber-hearth' | 'courtyard-hall';

export type MaterialSpec = {
  label: string;
  swatchHex: string;
};

export type Commission = {
  slug: 'fluted-master-suite' | 'formal-drawing-room' | 'heritage-panelled-gallery';
  title: string;
  scope: string;
  focus: string;
  typology: 'Private Residence' | 'Formal Reception' | 'Private Gallery';
  location: 'Biratnagar' | 'Dharan';
  year: '2024' | '2023';
  summary: string;
  image: ImageMetadata;
  primaryObjectPosition?: string;
  detailImage?: ImageMetadata;
  detailObjectPosition?: string;
  detailScale?: number;
  alt: string;
  materials: MaterialSpec[];
  narrative: { heading: string; text: string }[];
};

export type MaterialStudyNote = {
  key: GlbPinKey;
  title: string;
  category: string;
  summary: string;
  image: ImageMetadata;
  alt: string;
  materials: string[];
};

  alt: 'Stone studio table arranged with fluted timber sample, matte stone tiles, brushed brass hardware, woven linen swatch, and architectural plans',
};

export type ShowroomWaypoint = {
  id: string;
  index: string;
  label: string;
  u: number;
  cam: [number, number, number];
  target: [number, number, number];
  pin?: {
    anchor: [number, number, number];
    studySlug: GlbPinKey;
  };
};

export type ShowroomAsset =
  | {
      type: 'glb';
      src: string;
      mobileSrc?: string;
      cameraPathsSrc?: string;
      toneMapping: 'aces' | 'none';
      railFrom?: 'config' | 'empties';
    }
  | {
      type: 'splat';
      src: string;
      format: 'spz' | 'ply';
      toneMapping: 'none' | 'aces';
      transform?: {
        position?: [number, number, number];
        rotation?: [number, number, number];
        scale?: number;
      };
    };

export type ShowroomConfig = {
  label?: string;
  trackViewports: number;
  baseFov: number;
  asset: ShowroomAsset;
  fog: {
    color: string;
    near: number;
    far: number;
  };
  waypoints: ShowroomWaypoint[];
};

--- src\engine\gl\index.ts ---

import { Renderer, Camera, Transform, Plane, Program, Mesh, Texture, Vec2, Triangle } from 'ogl';
import { gsap } from 'gsap';
import { vertex, imageFragment, proceduralFragment } from './shaders';
import { scrollState } from '../scroll';
import { prefersReducedMotion } from '../env';

type Item = {
  el: HTMLElement;
  mesh: Mesh;
  program: Program;
  hover: { v: number };
  revealed: boolean;
  inView: boolean;
  scrub: boolean;
  io?: IntersectionObserver;
  visIo?: IntersectionObserver;
  off: () => void;
};

let renderer: Renderer | null = null;
let camera: Camera;
let scene: Transform;
let geometry: Plane;
const items: Item[] = [];
const textures = new Map<string, Texture>();
let vw = 0, vh = 0, time = 0, velocity = 0, lastBgRender = 0;
let paused = false;
let bg: { scene: Transform; program: Program } | null = null;
const mouse = { x: 0.5, y: 0.5, tx: 0.5, ty: 0.5 };

const hex = (h: string) => {
  const n = parseInt(h.replace('#', ''), 16);
  return [((n >> 16) & 255) / 255, ((n >> 8) & 255) / 255, (n & 255) / 255];
};

export function glSupported() {
  if (prefersReducedMotion()) return false;
  if ((navigator as Navigator & { connection?: { saveData?: boolean } }).connection?.saveData) return false;
  try { return !!document.createElement('canvas').getContext('webgl'); } catch { return false; }
}

export function initGL() {
  if (renderer || !glSupported()) return;
  renderer = new Renderer({
    dpr: Math.min(devicePixelRatio, 1.0),
    alpha: true,
    antialias: false,
    premultipliedAlpha: true,
  });
  const gl = renderer.gl;
  gl.canvas.classList.add('gl-canvas');
  gl.canvas.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
  (document.getElementById('gl') || document.body).appendChild(gl.canvas);
  camera = new Camera(gl, { near: -10, far: 10 });
  camera.position.z = 1;
  scene = new Transform();
  geometry = new Plane(gl, { widthSegments: 24, heightSegments: 24 });
  document.documentElement.classList.add('gl-on');
  resize();
  addEventListener('resize', resize);
  addEventListener('pointermove', (e) => { mouse.tx = e.clientX / vw; mouse.ty = 1 - e.clientY / vh; }, { passive: true });
  gsap.ticker.add(render);
}

export function setBackground(fragment: string, uniforms: Record<string, { value: unknown }> = {}) {
  if (!renderer) return null;
  clearBackground();
  const gl = renderer.gl;
  const program = new Program(gl, {
    vertex: `attribute vec2 uv; attribute vec2 position; varying vec2 vUv; void main() { vUv = uv; gl_Position = vec4(position, 0.0, 1.0); }`,
    fragment,
    uniforms: {
      uTime: { value: 0 },
      uResolution: { value: new Vec2(vw, vh) },
      uScroll: { value: 0 },
      uScrollY: { value: 0 },
      uVelocity: { value: 0 },
      uMouse: { value: new Vec2(0.5, 0.5) },
      ...uniforms,
    },
    depthTest: false,
    depthWrite: false,
  });
  const bgScene = new Transform();
  new Mesh(gl, { geometry: new Triangle(gl), program }).setParent(bgScene);
  bg = { scene: bgScene, program };
  return program;
}

export function clearBackground() { bg = null; }

function resize() {
  if (!renderer) return;
  vw = innerWidth; vh = innerHeight;
  renderer.setSize(vw, vh);
  camera.orthographic({ left: -vw / 2, right: vw / 2, top: vh / 2, bottom: -vh / 2, near: -10, far: 10 });
}

function loadTexture(img: HTMLImageElement, program: Program) {
  const src = img.currentSrc || img.src;
  const gl = renderer!.gl;
  let tex = textures.get(src);
  const apply = (t: Texture) => {
    program.uniforms.tMap.value = t;
    const im = t.image as HTMLImageElement;
    program.uniforms.uImage.value.set(im.naturalWidth || 1, im.naturalHeight || 1);
  };
  if (tex) return apply(tex);
  tex = new Texture(gl, { generateMipmaps: false, minFilter: gl.LINEAR });
  textures.set(src, tex);
  const im = new Image();
  im.crossOrigin = 'anonymous';
  im.decoding = 'async';
  im.onload = () => { tex!.image = im; apply(tex!); };
  im.src = src;
}

function add(el: HTMLElement) {
  if (!renderer) return;
  const gl = renderer.gl;
  const procedural = el.dataset.gl === 'procedural';
  const uniforms: Record<string, { value: unknown }> = {
    uPlane: { value: new Vec2(1, 1) },
    uTime: { value: 0 },
    uHover: { value: 0 },
    uVelocity: { value: 0 },
    uReveal: { value: el.hasAttribute('data-gl-instant') ? 1 : 0 },
    uMouse: { value: new Vec2(0.5, 0.5) },
  };
  if (procedural) {
    const [c1, c2, c3] = (el.dataset.colors || '#eceae4,#a67c52,#1f4a3c').split(',').map(hex);
    Object.assign(uniforms, { uC1: { value: c1 }, uC2: { value: c2 }, uC3: { value: c3 }, uSeed: { value: parseFloat(el.dataset.seed || '1') } });
  } else {
    const blank = new Texture(gl, { image: new Uint8Array([0, 0, 0, 0]), width: 1, height: 1 });
    Object.assign(uniforms, { tMap: { value: blank }, uImage: { value: new Vec2(1, 1) } });
  }
  const program = new Program(gl, { vertex, fragment: procedural ? proceduralFragment : imageFragment, uniforms, transparent: true });
  const mesh = new Mesh(gl, { geometry, program });
  mesh.setParent(scene);
  const img = el.querySelector('img');
  if (img && !procedural) {
    if (img.complete && img.naturalWidth) loadTexture(img, program);
    else img.addEventListener('load', () => loadTexture(img, program), { once: true });
  }
  const hover = { v: 0 };
  const enter = () => gsap.to(hover, { v: 1, duration: 0.9, ease: 'expo.out' });
  const leave = () => gsap.to(hover, { v: 0, duration: 0.9, ease: 'expo.out' });
  const move = (e: PointerEvent) => {
    const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
    program.uniforms.uMouse.value.set((e.clientX - r.left) / r.width, 1 - (e.clientY - r.top) / r.height);
  };
  el.addEventListener('pointerenter', enter);
  el.addEventListener('pointerleave', leave);
  el.addEventListener('pointermove', move);
  const scrub = el.hasAttribute('data-gl-scrub');
  const item: Item = {
    el, mesh, program, hover, revealed: false, inView: true, scrub,
    off: () => {
      el.removeEventListener('pointerenter', enter);
      el.removeEventListener('pointerleave', leave);
      el.removeEventListener('pointermove', move);
    },
  };
  item.visIo = new IntersectionObserver(([e]) => {
    item.inView = Boolean(e?.isIntersecting);
  }, { rootMargin: '200px 0px' });
  item.visIo.observe(el);
  if (!el.hasAttribute('data-gl-instant') && !scrub) {
    item.io = new IntersectionObserver(([e]) => {
      if (e.isIntersecting && !item.revealed) {
        item.revealed = true;
        gsap.to(program.uniforms.uReveal, { value: 1, duration: parseFloat(el.dataset.glDuration || '1.5'), ease: 'expo.out', delay: parseFloat(el.dataset.delay || '0') });
        item.io?.disconnect();
      }
    }, { rootMargin: '0px 0px -10% 0px' });
    item.io.observe(el);
  }
  items.push(item);
}

export function mountGL(root: ParentNode = document) {
  if (!renderer) return;
  root.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[data-gl]').forEach(add);
}

export function unmountGL() {
  while (items.length) {
    const it = items.pop()!;
    it.off();
    it.io?.disconnect();
    it.visIo?.disconnect();
    it.mesh.setParent(null);
    it.program.remove?.();
  }
}

export function hideGL(duration = 0.55) {
  return gsap.to(items.map((i) => i.program.uniforms.uReveal), { value: 0, duration, ease: 'expo.in' });
}

export function pauseGL() {
  paused = true;
  if (renderer) renderer.gl.canvas.style.visibility = 'hidden';
}

export function resumeGL() {
  paused = false;
  if (renderer) renderer.gl.canvas.style.visibility = '';
}

function render(t: number) {
  if (!renderer || paused || document.hidden) return;
  time = t;
  const sNow = scrollState();
  const target = Math.max(-40, Math.min(40, sNow.velocity));
  velocity += (target - velocity) * 0.12;
  let visible = 0;
  for (const it of items) {
    if (!it.inView) {
      it.mesh.visible = false;
      continue;
    }
    const r = it.el.getBoundingClientRect();
    const onScreen = r.bottom > -100 && r.top < vh + 100 && r.width > 0;
    it.mesh.visible = onScreen;
    if (!onScreen) continue;
    visible++;
    it.mesh.scale.set(r.width, r.height, 1);
    it.mesh.position.set(r.left + r.width / 2 - vw / 2, vh / 2 - (r.top + r.height / 2), 0);
    const u = it.program.uniforms;
    u.uPlane.value.set(r.width, r.height);
    u.uTime.value = time;
    u.uHover.value = it.hover.v;
    u.uVelocity.value = velocity * 0.05;
    if (it.scrub) u.uReveal.value = Math.min(1, Math.max(0, (vh - r.top) / (vh * 0.75)));
  }
  mouse.x += (mouse.tx - mouse.x) * 0.08;
  mouse.y += (mouse.ty - mouse.y) * 0.08;
  if (bg) {
    if (
      visible === 0 &&
      Math.abs(sNow.velocity) < 0.05 &&
      Math.abs(mouse.tx - mouse.x) < 0.002 &&
      Math.abs(mouse.ty - mouse.y) < 0.002 &&
      t - lastBgRender < 0.04
    ) {
      return;
    }
    lastBgRender = t;
    const u = bg.program.uniforms;
    u.uTime.value = time;
    u.uResolution.value.set(vw, vh);
    u.uScroll.value = sNow.progress;
    u.uScrollY.value = sNow.y;
    u.uVelocity.value = velocity;
    u.uMouse.value.set(mouse.x, mouse.y);
    renderer.render({ scene: bg.scene, camera });
    renderer.render({ scene, camera, clear: false });
  } else if (visible || renderer.gl.canvas.dataset.dirty !== '0') {
    renderer.render({ scene, camera });
    renderer.gl.canvas.dataset.dirty = visible ? '1' : '0';
  }
}

--- src\engine\gl\shaders.ts ---

export const vertex = /* glsl */ `
attribute vec3 position;
attribute vec2 uv;
uniform mat4 modelViewMatrix;
uniform mat4 projectionMatrix;
uniform float uVelocity;
uniform float uHover;
varying vec2 vUv;
void main() {
  vUv = uv;
  vec3 p = position;
  float bend = sin(uv.x * 3.14159265);
  p.y += bend * uVelocity * 0.085;
  p.z += bend * (uHover * 0.05 + abs(uVelocity) * 0.025);
  gl_Position = projectionMatrix * modelViewMatrix * vec4(p, 1.0);
}`;

const common = /* glsl */ `
precision highp float;
varying vec2 vUv;
uniform vec2 uPlane;
uniform float uTime;
uniform float uHover;
uniform float uVelocity;
uniform float uReveal;
uniform vec2 uMouse;
float hash(vec2 p) { return fract(sin(dot(p, vec2(127.1, 311.7))) * 43758.5453); }
float noise(vec2 p) {
  vec2 i = floor(p), f = fract(p);
  vec2 u = f * f * (3.0 - 2.0 * f);
  return mix(mix(hash(i), hash(i + vec2(1.0, 0.0)), u.x), mix(hash(i + vec2(0.0, 1.0)), hash(i + vec2(1.0, 1.0)), u.x), u.y);
}
float fbm(vec2 p) {
  float v = 0.0, a = 0.5;
  for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) { v += a * noise(p); p *= 2.02; a *= 0.5; }
  return v;
}
float revealMask(vec2 uv) {
  float edge = uReveal * 1.24 - 0.12 + (noise(uv * vec2(6.0, 2.0) + uTime * 0.16) - 0.5) * 0.1;
  return smoothstep(edge, edge - 0.07, uv.y);
}`;

// Optical architectural daylight plate with filmic gallery curves, chromatic split & hover relief
export const imageFragment = /* glsl */ `
${common}
uniform sampler2D tMap;
uniform vec2 uImage;
vec2 cover(vec2 uv) {
  vec2 r = vec2(min((uPlane.x / uPlane.y) / (uImage.x / uImage.y), 1.0), min((uPlane.y / uPlane.x) / (uImage.y / uImage.x), 1.0));
  return uv * r + (1.0 - r) * 0.5;
}
void main() {
  vec2 uv = vUv;
  vec2 c = uv - 0.5;
  uv = 0.5 + c * (1.0 - uHover * 0.05);
  float liq = (fbm(uv * 3.2 + uTime * 0.22) - 0.5) * (0.022 * uHover + min(0.016, abs(uVelocity) * 0.007));
  uv += liq;
  vec2 dir = (uv - uMouse) * uHover;
  uv += dir * 0.028 * smoothstep(0.55, 0.0, length(vUv - uMouse));
  float shift = min(0.014, abs(uVelocity) * 0.0045 + uHover * 0.003);
  vec2 t = cover(uv);
  float r = texture2D(tMap, t + vec2(shift, 0.0)).r;
  float g = texture2D(tMap, t).g;
  float b = texture2D(tMap, t - vec2(shift, 0.0)).b;
  vec3 col = vec3(r, g, b);
  // Subtle filmic gallery S-curve + warm architectural limestone harmony
  col = mix(col, col * col * (3.0 - 2.0 * col), 0.22);
  col += (hash(vUv * uPlane + fract(uTime)) - 0.5) * 0.018;
  gl_FragColor = vec4(col, 1.0) * revealMask(vUv);
}`;

export const proceduralFragment = /* glsl */ `
${common}
uniform vec3 uC1;
uniform vec3 uC2;
uniform vec3 uC3;
uniform float uSeed;
void main() {
  vec2 uv = vUv;
  vec2 p = uv * vec2(uPlane.x / uPlane.y, 1.0) * 1.8 + uSeed * 7.0;
  float t = uTime * 0.04;
  vec2 q = vec2(fbm(p + t), fbm(p + vec2(5.2, 1.3) - t));
  float f = fbm(p + 2.2 * q);
  vec3 col = mix(uC1, uC2, smoothstep(0.2, 0.8, f));
  col = mix(col, uC3, smoothstep(0.55, 0.95, f));
  col += (hash(uv * uPlane + fract(uTime)) - 0.5) * 0.03;
  gl_FragColor = vec4(col, 1.0) * revealMask(vUv);
}`;

--- src\engine\showroom\assets.ts ---

import * as THREE from 'three';
import { GLTFLoader } from 'three/examples/jsm/loaders/GLTFLoader.js';
import { DRACOLoader } from 'three/examples/jsm/loaders/DRACOLoader.js';
import type { ShowroomAsset, ShowroomWaypoint } from '../../data/site';
import { isMobile } from '../env';

export type LoadedAsset = {
  object3D: THREE.Object3D;
  toneMapping: THREE.ToneMapping;
  extractedWaypoints?: ShowroomWaypoint[];
  dispose: () => void;
};

type LoadOptions = {
  asset: ShowroomAsset;
  waypoints: ShowroomWaypoint[];
  scene: THREE.Scene;
  renderer: THREE.WebGLRenderer;
  onProgress?: (fraction: number, label: string) => void;
};

function disposeObjectTree(root: THREE.Object3D) {
  root.traverse((node) => {
    const mesh = node as THREE.Mesh;
    if (mesh.geometry) {
      mesh.geometry.dispose();
    }
    if (mesh.material) {
      const mats = Array.isArray(mesh.material) ? mesh.material : [mesh.material];
      for (const m of mats) {
        for (const key of Object.keys(m)) {
          const val = (m as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)[key];
          if (val && typeof val === 'object' && 'isTexture' in val) {
            (val as THREE.Texture).dispose();
          }
        }
        m.dispose();
      }
    }
  });
}

function resolveToneMapping(mode: 'aces' | 'none'): THREE.ToneMapping {
  return mode === 'aces' ? THREE.ACESFilmicToneMapping : THREE.NoToneMapping;
}

function attachVideoWall(root: THREE.Object3D): () => void {
  let targetMesh: THREE.Mesh | null = null;
  root.traverse((obj) => {
    if ((obj as THREE.Mesh).isMesh && /VideoWall_Vault/i.test(obj.name)) {
      targetMesh = obj as THREE.Mesh;
    }
  });
  if (!targetMesh) return () => {};

  const video = document.createElement('video');
  video.src = '/videos/rubiconius-vault.mp4';
  video.crossOrigin = 'anonymous';
  video.loop = true;
  video.muted = true;
  video.playsInline = true;
  video.autoplay = true;

  const onCanPlay = () => {
    video.play().catch(() => {});
  };
  video.addEventListener('canplay', onCanPlay);

  const tex = new THREE.VideoTexture(video);
  tex.colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
  tex.minFilter = THREE.LinearFilter;
  tex.magFilter = THREE.LinearFilter;

  const prevMat = (targetMesh as THREE.Mesh).material;
  const videoMat = new THREE.MeshBasicMaterial({ map: tex, side: THREE.DoubleSide });
  (targetMesh as THREE.Mesh).material = videoMat;
  video.play().catch(() => {});

  return () => {
    video.removeEventListener('canplay', onCanPlay);
    video.pause();
    video.removeAttribute('src');
    video.load();
    tex.dispose();
    videoMat.dispose();
    if (prevMat) {
      const arr = Array.isArray(prevMat) ? prevMat : [prevMat];
      arr.forEach((m) => m.dispose());
    }
  };
}

function extractWaypointsFromScene(
  sceneRoot: THREE.Object3D,
  fallbackWaypoints: ShowroomWaypoint[]
): ShowroomWaypoint[] {
  const camMap = new Map<number, [number, number, number]>();
  const tgtMap = new Map<number, [number, number, number]>();
  const pinMap = new Map<string, [number, number, number]>();
  const tmp = new THREE.Vector3();

  sceneRoot.updateMatrixWorld(true);
  sceneRoot.traverse((obj) => {
    const mCam = obj.name.match(/^CamPoint_(\d+)$/i);
    if (mCam) {
      obj.getWorldPosition(tmp);
      camMap.set(parseInt(mCam[1], 10), [tmp.x, tmp.y, tmp.z]);
    }
    const mTgt = obj.name.match(/^CamTarget_(\d+)$/i);
    if (mTgt) {
      obj.getWorldPosition(tmp);
      tgtMap.set(parseInt(mTgt[1], 10), [tmp.x, tmp.y, tmp.z]);
    }
    if (/^Hotspot_/i.test(obj.name)) {
      obj.getWorldPosition(tmp);
      pinMap.set(obj.name.toLowerCase(), [tmp.x, tmp.y, tmp.z]);
    }
  });

  const slugToEmpty: Record<string, string> = {
    'fluted-suite': 'hotspot_flutedsuite',
    'timber-hearth': 'hotspot_timberhearth',
    'courtyard-hall': 'hotspot_courtyardhall',
  };

  return fallbackWaypoints.map((wp, idx) => {
    const cam = camMap.get(idx) ?? wp.cam;
    const target = tgtMap.get(idx) ?? wp.target;
    let pin = wp.pin;
    if (pin) {
      const emptyKey = slugToEmpty[pin.studySlug];
      const extractedAnchor = emptyKey ? pinMap.get(emptyKey) : undefined;
      if (extractedAnchor) {
        pin = { ...pin, anchor: extractedAnchor };
      }
    }
    return { ...wp, cam, target, pin };
  });
}

async function loadGlbAsset(opts: LoadOptions & { asset: Extract<ShowroomAsset, { type: 'glb' }> }): Promise<LoadedAsset> {
  const { asset, waypoints, scene, onProgress } = opts;

  const draco = new DRACOLoader();
  draco.setDecoderPath('/draco/');

  const loader = new GLTFLoader();
  loader.setDRACOLoader(draco);

  const saveData = (navigator as Navigator & { connection?: { saveData?: boolean } }).connection?.saveData;
  const chosenSrc = (isMobile() || saveData) && asset.mobileSrc ? asset.mobileSrc : asset.src;

  let extractedWaypoints = waypoints;

  if (asset.railFrom === 'empties' && asset.cameraPathsSrc) {
    onProgress?.(0.15, 'Loading camera rail');
    try {
      const camGltf = await loader.loadAsync(asset.cameraPathsSrc);
      extractedWaypoints = extractWaypointsFromScene(camGltf.scene, waypoints);
      disposeObjectTree(camGltf.scene);
    } catch {
      // Fallback cleanly to configured waypoints in site.ts
    }
  }

  onProgress?.(0.3, 'Streaming architectural scene');
  const gltf = await loader.loadAsync(chosenSrc, (ev) => {
    if (ev.total > 0) {
      const frac = 0.3 + Math.min(0.65, (ev.loaded / ev.total) * 0.65);
      onProgress?.(frac, 'Streaming architectural scene');
    }
  });

  const root = gltf.scene;
  root.traverse((child) => {
    const mesh = child as THREE.Mesh;
    if (mesh.isMesh && mesh.material) {
      const mats = Array.isArray(mesh.material) ? mesh.material : [mesh.material];
      for (const m of mats) {
        if ('map' in m && m.map) {
          (m.map as THREE.Texture).colorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
          (m.map as THREE.Texture).anisotropy = Math.min(8, opts.renderer.capabilities.getMaxAnisotropy());
        }
        if ('fog' in m) {
          (m as THREE.MeshBasicMaterial).fog = true;
        }
      }
    }
  });

  const disposeVideo = attachVideoWall(root);
  scene.add(root);
  onProgress?.(1, 'Scene ready');

  return {
    object3D: root,
    toneMapping: resolveToneMapping(asset.toneMapping),
    extractedWaypoints,
    dispose: () => {
      disposeVideo();
      scene.remove(root);
      disposeObjectTree(root);
      draco.dispose();
    },
  };
}

async function loadSplatAsset(opts: LoadOptions & { asset: Extract<ShowroomAsset, { type: 'splat' }> }): Promise<LoadedAsset> {
  const { asset, scene, renderer, onProgress } = opts;
  onProgress?.(0.2, 'Initializing Gaussian Splat engine');

  // Lazy-loaded so GLB builds pay zero Spark bundle cost on initial load
  const { SparkRenderer, SplatMesh, SplatFileType } = await import('@sparkjsdev/spark');

  const spark = new SparkRenderer({ renderer });
  scene.add(spark);

  onProgress?.(0.45, 'Streaming 3D Gaussian Splat');
  const fileType = asset.format === 'ply' ? SplatFileType.PLY : SplatFileType.SPZ;
  const splat = new SplatMesh({
    url: asset.src,
    fileType,
  });

  if (asset.transform?.position) {
    splat.position.set(...asset.transform.position);
  }
  if (asset.transform?.rotation) {
    splat.rotation.set(...asset.transform.rotation);
  }
  if (typeof asset.transform?.scale === 'number') {
    splat.scale.setScalar(asset.transform.scale);
  }

  scene.add(splat);
  await splat.initialized;
  onProgress?.(1, 'Splat ready');

  return {
    object3D: splat,
    toneMapping: resolveToneMapping(asset.toneMapping),
    dispose: () => {
      scene.remove(splat);
      scene.remove(spark);
      splat.dispose();
      if (typeof (spark as unknown as { dispose?: () => void }).dispose === 'function') {
        (spark as unknown as { dispose: () => void }).dispose();
      }
    },
  };
}

export async function loadShowroomAsset(opts: LoadOptions): Promise<LoadedAsset> {
  if (opts.asset.type === 'splat') {
    return loadSplatAsset({ ...opts, asset: opts.asset });
  }
  return loadGlbAsset({ ...opts, asset: opts.asset });
}

--- src\engine\showroom\atmosphere.ts ---

import * as THREE from 'three';
import { RoomEnvironment } from 'three/examples/jsm/environments/RoomEnvironment.js';

export type LivingAtmosphere = {
  update: (timeSec: number) => void;
  attachToLoadedScene: (root: THREE.Object3D) => void;
  dispose: () => void;
};

const LEAF_SHADOW_VERT = /* glsl */ `
varying vec2 vUv;
void main() {
  vUv = uv;
  gl_Position = projectionMatrix * modelViewMatrix * vec4(position, 1.0);
}
`;

// Feathered NormalBlending dappled botanical leaf shadow shader:
// Zero quad boundary edge (feathers to alpha 0.000 well before edges) & zero MultiplyBlending console warnings!
const LEAF_SHADOW_FRAG = /* glsl */ `
precision highp float;
varying vec2 vUv;
uniform float uTime;

float hash(vec2 p) {
  return fract(sin(dot(p, vec2(127.1, 311.7))) * 43758.5453123);
}

float noise(vec2 p) {
  vec2 i = floor(p);
  vec2 f = fract(p);
  vec2 u = f * f * (3.0 - 2.0 * f);
  return mix(
    mix(hash(i), hash(i + vec2(1.0, 0.0)), u.x),
    mix(hash(i + vec2(0.0, 1.0)), hash(i + vec2(1.0, 1.0)), u.x),
    u.y
  );
}

float fbm(vec2 p) {
  float v = 0.0;
  float a = 0.5;
  mat2 rot = mat2(0.8, 0.6, -0.6, 0.8);
  for (int i = 0; i < 4; i++) {
    v += a * noise(p);
    p = rot * p * 2.04;
    a *= 0.5;
  }
  return v;
}

void main() {
  // Wide elliptical feather reaches 0.0 well inside the UV quad -> zero harsh plane seam ever!
  vec2 c = (vUv - 0.5) * 2.0;
  float radial = smoothstep(0.92, 0.18, length(c));
  if (radial <= 0.001) discard;

  float swayX = sin(uTime * 0.42 + vUv.y * 3.1) * 0.10 + cos(uTime * 0.23) * 0.04;
  float swayY = cos(uTime * 0.36 + vUv.x * 2.7) * 0.08;

  vec2 p = vec2(vUv.x * 4.8 + vUv.y * 2.2 + swayX, vUv.y * 7.2 - vUv.x * 1.3 + swayY);
  float c1 = fbm(p);
  float c2 = fbm(p * 1.8 + vec2(-uTime * 0.05, uTime * 0.035));
  float fronds = smoothstep(0.42, 0.76, c1 * 0.62 + c2 * 0.46);

  float alpha = fronds * radial * 0.16;
  gl_FragColor = vec4(0.14, 0.12, 0.10, alpha);
}
`;

const DUST_VERT = /* glsl */ `
attribute float aScale;
attribute float aPhase;
uniform float uTime;
uniform float uDpr;
varying float vAlpha;

void main() {
  vec3 pos = position;
  pos.x += sin(uTime * 0.26 + aPhase) * 0.16;
  pos.y += cos(uTime * 0.20 + aPhase * 1.3) * 0.10;
  pos.z += sin(uTime * 0.18 + aPhase * 0.7) * 0.14;

  vec4 mv = modelViewMatrix * vec4(pos, 1.0);
  float dist = max(1.0, -mv.z);
  gl_PointSize = clamp((aScale * 14.0 * uDpr) / dist, 1.5, 7.0);
  vAlpha = smoothstep(10.0, 2.2, dist) * (0.22 + 0.14 * sin(uTime * 0.85 + aPhase));
  gl_Position = projectionMatrix * mv;
}
`;

const DUST_FRAG = /* glsl */ `
precision mediump float;
varying float vAlpha;

void main() {
  float d = length(gl_PointCoord - 0.5);
  float soft = smoothstep(0.5, 0.1, d);
  gl_FragColor = vec4(1.0, 0.94, 0.83, soft * vAlpha);
}
`;

const WATER_VERT = /* glsl */ `
varying vec2 vUv;
varying vec3 vWorldPos;
void main() {
  vUv = uv;
  vec4 wp = modelMatrix * vec4(position, 1.0);
  vWorldPos = wp.xyz;
  gl_Position = projectionMatrix * viewMatrix * wp;
}
`;

// Translucent Architectural Reflecting Basin Shader:
// Reveals the dark wet basalt pool bed & submerged paver plinths underneath while reflecting golden sky & ripples
const WATER_FRAG = /* glsl */ `
precision highp float;
varying vec2 vUv;
varying vec3 vWorldPos;
uniform float uTime;

float waveHeight(vec2 p, float t) {
  float w = 0.0;
  w += sin(p.x * 2.1 + t * 0.9 + cos(p.y * 1.5 - t * 0.5)) * 0.45;
  w += cos(p.y * 2.8 - t * 0.75 + sin(p.x * 1.9 + t * 0.6)) * 0.35;
  w += sin((p.x + p.y) * 4.2 + t * 1.2) * 0.20;
  return w;
}

void main() {
  vec2 p = vUv * 10.0;
  float eps = 0.06;
  float h0 = waveHeight(p, uTime);
  float hx = waveHeight(p + vec2(eps, 0.0), uTime);
  float hy = waveHeight(p + vec2(0.0, eps), uTime);
  vec3 N = normalize(vec3((h0 - hx) * 0.18, 1.0, (h0 - hy) * 0.18));

  vec3 V = normalize(cameraPosition - vWorldPos);
  float ndotv = clamp(dot(N, V), 0.0, 1.0);
  // Physical Schlick Fresnel: glancing angles reflect luminous sky, downward views stay translucent to dark basalt bed
  float fresnel = 0.08 + 0.88 * pow(1.0 - ndotv, 3.2);

  // Reflected morning sky gradient + subtle vertical architectural fin reflection
  vec3 R = reflect(-V, N);
  vec3 skyHorizon = vec3(0.92, 0.87, 0.77);
  vec3 skyZenith  = vec3(0.68, 0.75, 0.79);
  vec3 refl = mix(skyHorizon, skyZenith, clamp(R.y * 1.4, 0.0, 1.0));
  float finRefl = 0.90 + 0.10 * sin(vWorldPos.x * 14.0 + N.x * 8.0);
  refl *= finRefl;

  // Sun specular sparkle on micro-ripples
  vec3 sunDir = normalize(vec3(0.45, 0.65, -0.60));
  vec3 H = normalize(sunDir + V);
  float spec = pow(max(0.0, dot(N, H)), 96.0) * 0.55;

  // Delicate underwater caustic web
  float caustic = smoothstep(0.55, 0.95, abs(h0)) * 0.12;
  vec3 deepWater = vec3(0.04, 0.085, 0.078) + vec3(0.85, 0.80, 0.68) * caustic;

  vec3 col = mix(deepWater, refl, fresnel) + vec3(1.0, 0.95, 0.84) * spec;
  float alpha = clamp(0.42 + fresnel * 0.44, 0.42, 0.84);
  gl_FragColor = vec4(col, alpha);
}
`;

export function createLivingAtmosphere(
  scene: THREE.Scene,
  renderer: THREE.WebGLRenderer
): LivingAtmosphere {
  const disposables: Array<() => void> = [];

  // 1. Live PMREM Studio Environment Map for metallic brass, bronze & Poly Haven PBR props
  const pmrem = new THREE.PMREMGenerator(renderer);
  const envTex = pmrem.fromScene(new RoomEnvironment(), 0.04).texture;
  scene.environment = envTex;
  scene.environmentIntensity = 0.78;
  pmrem.dispose();

  // Warm balanced architectural lighting for live PBR props (mid_century_lounge_chair, potted plants, vases)
  const hemi = new THREE.HemisphereLight(0xfff4e2, 0x3b352c, 0.95);
  const key = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffe9c6, 1.55);
  key.position.set(6, 9, 3);
  const fill = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xdce6ee, 0.45);
  fill.position.set(-5, 4, -4);
  scene.add(hemi, key, fill);
  disposables.push(() => {
    scene.remove(hemi, key, fill);
    scene.environment = null;
    envTex.dispose();
  });

  // 2. Feathered Swaying Botanical Leaf Shadow Patches (NormalBlending -> zero seams, zero console errors)
  const leafUniforms = { uTime: { value: 0 } };
  const leafMat = new THREE.ShaderMaterial({
    vertexShader: LEAF_SHADOW_VERT,
    fragmentShader: LEAF_SHADOW_FRAG,
    uniforms: leafUniforms,
    transparent: true,
    depthWrite: false,
    blending: THREE.NormalBlending,
  });

  const shadowPatches: Array<{ pos: [number, number, number]; size: [number, number] }> = [
    { pos: [1.3, 0.008, 4.9], size: [4.6, 3.8] },
    { pos: [0.0, 0.008, -5.6], size: [4.6, 3.8] },
  ];

  const shadowMeshes: THREE.Mesh[] = [];
  for (const patch of shadowPatches) {
    const geo = new THREE.PlaneGeometry(patch.size[0], patch.size[1]);
    geo.rotateX(-Math.PI / 2);
    const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(geo, leafMat);
    mesh.position.set(...patch.pos);
    mesh.renderOrder = 2;
    scene.add(mesh);
    shadowMeshes.push(mesh);
  }
  disposables.push(() => {
    for (const m of shadowMeshes) {
      scene.remove(m);
      m.geometry.dispose();
    }
    leafMat.dispose();
  });

  // 3. Whisper-Soft Floating Golden Sunlight Dust Motes
  const count = 140;
  const positions = new Float32Array(count * 3);
  const scales = new Float32Array(count);
  const phases = new Float32Array(count);

  for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
    const inSkylight = i >= count / 2;
    if (inSkylight) {
      positions[i * 3 + 0] = (Math.random() - 0.5) * 3.2;
      positions[i * 3 + 1] = 0.5 + Math.random() * 3.2;
      positions[i * 3 + 2] = -5.7 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 3.0;
    } else {
      positions[i * 3 + 0] = -1.0 + Math.random() * 4.4;
      positions[i * 3 + 1] = 0.5 + Math.random() * 3.0;
      positions[i * 3 + 2] = 5.4 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 4.0;
    }
    scales[i] = 0.5 + Math.random() * 1.05;
    phases[i] = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2;
  }

  const dustGeo = new THREE.BufferGeometry();
  dustGeo.setAttribute('position', new THREE.BufferAttribute(positions, 3));
  dustGeo.setAttribute('aScale', new THREE.BufferAttribute(scales, 1));
  dustGeo.setAttribute('aPhase', new THREE.BufferAttribute(phases, 1));

  const dustUniforms = {
    uTime: { value: 0 },
    uDpr: { value: renderer.getPixelRatio() },
  };
  const dustMat = new THREE.ShaderMaterial({
    vertexShader: DUST_VERT,
    fragmentShader: DUST_FRAG,
    uniforms: dustUniforms,
    transparent: true,
    depthWrite: false,
    blending: THREE.AdditiveBlending,
  });
  const dustPts = new THREE.Points(dustGeo, dustMat);
  dustPts.frustumCulled = false;
  scene.add(dustPts);
  disposables.push(() => {
    scene.remove(dustPts);
    dustGeo.dispose();
    dustMat.dispose();
  });

  // 4. Translucent Shimmering Courtyard Water + Subtle Pendant Sway
  const waterUniforms = { uTime: { value: 0 } };
  let waterMat: THREE.ShaderMaterial | null = null;
  const pendants: Array<{ obj: THREE.Object3D; baseRotZ: number; phase: number }> = [];

  const attachToLoadedScene = (root: THREE.Object3D) => {
    root.traverse((obj) => {
      if ((obj as THREE.Mesh).isMesh && /Courtyard_WaterPlane/i.test(obj.name)) {
        waterMat = new THREE.ShaderMaterial({
          vertexShader: WATER_VERT,
          fragmentShader: WATER_FRAG,
          uniforms: waterUniforms,
          transparent: true,
          depthWrite: false,
        });
        (obj as THREE.Mesh).material = waterMat;
        (obj as THREE.Mesh).renderOrder = 3;
      }
      if (/ST02_PendShade_/i.test(obj.name)) {
        pendants.push({
          obj,
          baseRotZ: obj.rotation.z,
          phase: pendants.length * 1.35,
        });
      }
    });
  };

  disposables.push(() => {
    waterMat?.dispose();
    pendants.length = 0;
  });

  return {
    attachToLoadedScene,
    update: (timeSec: number) => {
      leafUniforms.uTime.value = timeSec;
      dustUniforms.uTime.value = timeSec;
      waterUniforms.uTime.value = timeSec;
      for (const p of pendants) {
        p.obj.rotation.z = p.baseRotZ + Math.sin(timeSec * 0.72 + p.phase) * 0.007;
      }
    },
    dispose: () => {
      while (disposables.length) {
        disposables.pop()?.();
      }
    },
  };
}

--- src\engine\showroom\chapters.ts ---

import type { ShowroomWaypoint } from '../../data/site';
import { getScrollLimit, scrollToTarget } from '../scroll';
import { studioAudio } from '../audio';

export class ChapterBar {
  private waypoints: ShowroomWaypoint[];
  private buttons: HTMLButtonElement[];
  private fillEl: HTMLElement | null;
  private bannerEl: HTMLElement | null;
  private bannerIndexEl: HTMLElement | null;
  private bannerTitleEl: HTMLElement | null;
  private activeIdx = -1;
  private bannerTimer: number | null = null;
  private onJump?: () => void;
  private scrollTargetResolver?: (u: number) => number;

  constructor(
    root: HTMLElement,
    waypoints: ShowroomWaypoint[],
    signal: AbortSignal,
    onJump?: () => void,
    scrollTargetResolver?: (u: number) => number
  ) {
    this.waypoints = waypoints;
    this.onJump = onJump;
    this.scrollTargetResolver = scrollTargetResolver;
    this.buttons = [...root.querySelectorAll<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-chapter-btn]')];
    this.fillEl = root.querySelector('[data-chapter-fill]');
    this.bannerEl = root.querySelector('[data-room-banner]');
    this.bannerIndexEl = root.querySelector('[data-room-banner-index]');
    this.bannerTitleEl = root.querySelector('[data-room-banner-title]');

    this.buttons.forEach((btn, idx) => {
      btn.addEventListener(
        'click',
        () => {
          btn.blur();
          this.jumpToIndex(idx);
        },
        { signal }
      );
      btn.addEventListener(
        'keydown',
        (e) => {
          if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') {
            e.preventDefault();
            const next = Math.min(this.buttons.length - 1, idx + 1);
            this.buttons[next]?.focus();
            this.jumpToIndex(next);
          } else if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft') {
            e.preventDefault();
            const prev = Math.max(0, idx - 1);
            this.buttons[prev]?.focus();
            this.jumpToIndex(prev);
          }
        },
        { signal }
      );
    });

    signal.addEventListener(
      'abort',
      () => {
        if (this.bannerTimer !== null) window.clearTimeout(this.bannerTimer);
      },
      { once: true }
    );
  }

  public jumpToIndex(idx: number) {
    const wp = this.waypoints[idx];
    if (!wp) return;
    studioAudio.tick(560, 0.025);
    this.onJump?.();
    const targetY = this.scrollTargetResolver
      ? this.scrollTargetResolver(wp.u)
      : wp.u * getScrollLimit();
    scrollToTarget(targetY, { duration: 1.2 });
  }

  public update(progressU: number, isInitial = false) {
    if (this.fillEl) {
      this.fillEl.style.transform = `scaleX(${Math.max(0, Math.min(1, progressU))})`;
    }

    let nearestIdx = 0;
    let nearestDist = Infinity;
    for (let i = 0; i < this.waypoints.length; i++) {
      const d = Math.abs(progressU - this.waypoints[i].u);
      if (d < nearestDist) {
        nearestDist = d;
        nearestIdx = i;
      }
    }

    if (nearestIdx !== this.activeIdx) {
      this.activeIdx = nearestIdx;
      this.buttons.forEach((btn, i) => {
        const active = i === nearestIdx;
        btn.classList.toggle('is-active', active);
        if (active) btn.setAttribute('aria-current', 'step');
        else btn.removeAttribute('aria-current');
      });

      const wp = this.waypoints[nearestIdx];
      if (wp && !isInitial) {
        this.flashRoomBanner(wp);
      }
    }
  }

  private flashRoomBanner(wp: ShowroomWaypoint) {
    if (!this.bannerEl) return;
    if (this.bannerIndexEl) this.bannerIndexEl.textContent = wp.index;
    if (this.bannerTitleEl) this.bannerTitleEl.textContent = wp.label;

    this.bannerEl.classList.add('is-visible');
    if (this.bannerTimer !== null) window.clearTimeout(this.bannerTimer);
    this.bannerTimer = window.setTimeout(() => {
      this.bannerEl?.classList.remove('is-visible');
    }, 1750);
  }
}

--- src\engine\showroom\debug.ts ---

import * as THREE from 'three';

export class DebugFlyController {
  private camera: THREE.PerspectiveCamera;
  private canvas: HTMLCanvasElement;
  private keys = new Set<string>();
  private dragging = false;
  private lastX = 0;
  private lastY = 0;
  private yaw = 0;
  private pitch = 0;
  private badge: HTMLElement | null = null;
  private active = false;

  constructor(camera: THREE.PerspectiveCamera, canvas: HTMLCanvasElement, signal: AbortSignal) {
    this.camera = camera;
    this.canvas = canvas;

    const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
    const hasParam = params.get('debug') === '1';
    if (!hasParam) return;

    this.active = true;
    const euler = new THREE.Euler().setFromQuaternion(camera.quaternion, 'YXZ');
    this.yaw = euler.y;
    this.pitch = euler.x;

    this.mountBadge();
    window.addEventListener('keydown', this.onKeyDown, { signal });
    window.addEventListener('keyup', this.onKeyUp, { signal });
    this.canvas.addEventListener('pointerdown', this.onDown, { signal });
    window.addEventListener('pointermove', this.onMove, { passive: true, signal });
    window.addEventListener('pointerup', this.onUp, { passive: true, signal });
    signal.addEventListener('abort', () => this.badge?.remove(), { once: true });
  }

  public isActive() {
    return this.active;
  }

  private mountBadge() {
    const el = document.createElement('div');
    el.className = 'showroom-debug-badge';
    el.setAttribute('data-debug-badge', '');
    el.textContent = 'DEBUG FLY · WASD/QE move · Drag look · Press P to log waypoint';
    document.body.appendChild(el);
    this.badge = el;
  }

  private onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
    const k = e.key.toLowerCase();
    if (k === 'p') {
      this.logWaypoint();
      return;
    }
    this.keys.add(k);
  };

  private onKeyUp = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
    this.keys.delete(e.key.toLowerCase());
  };

  private onDown = (e: PointerEvent) => {
    if (e.button !== 0) return;
    this.dragging = true;
    this.lastX = e.clientX;
    this.lastY = e.clientY;
  };

  private onMove = (e: PointerEvent) => {
    if (!this.dragging) return;
    const dx = e.clientX - this.lastX;
    const dy = e.clientY - this.lastY;
    this.lastX = e.clientX;
    this.lastY = e.clientY;
    this.yaw -= dx * 0.004;
    this.pitch = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(this.pitch - dy * 0.004, -Math.PI * 0.48, Math.PI * 0.48);
  };

  private onUp = () => {
    this.dragging = false;
  };

  public logWaypoint() {
    const pos = this.camera.position;
    const dir = new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, -1).applyQuaternion(this.camera.quaternion);
    const tgt = pos.clone().addScaledVector(dir, 3.0);
    const r = (n: number) => Math.round(n * 1000) / 1000;
    const snippet = {
      cam: [r(pos.x), r(pos.y), r(pos.z)],
      target: [r(tgt.x), r(tgt.y), r(tgt.z)],
    };
    console.info('[Showroom Waypoint]', JSON.stringify(snippet));
    if (this.badge) {
      this.badge.textContent = `Logged waypoint: cam ${JSON.stringify(snippet.cam)} target ${JSON.stringify(snippet.target)}`;
    }
    return snippet;
  }

  public update(dt: number) {
    if (!this.active) return;
    this.camera.quaternion.setFromEuler(new THREE.Euler(this.pitch, this.yaw, 0, 'YXZ'));

    const speed = (this.keys.has('shift') ? 5.5 : 2.2) * dt;
    const forward = new THREE.Vector3(0, 0, -1).applyQuaternion(this.camera.quaternion);
    const right = new THREE.Vector3(1, 0, 0).applyQuaternion(this.camera.quaternion);

    if (this.keys.has('w')) this.camera.position.addScaledVector(forward, speed);
    if (this.keys.has('s')) this.camera.position.addScaledVector(forward, -speed);
    if (this.keys.has('d')) this.camera.position.addScaledVector(right, speed);
    if (this.keys.has('a')) this.camera.position.addScaledVector(right, -speed);
    if (this.keys.has('e')) this.camera.position.y += speed;
    if (this.keys.has('q')) this.camera.position.y -= speed;
  }
}

--- src\engine\showroom\drawer.ts ---

import { materialStudies, site, type MaterialStudyNote } from '../../data/site';
import { startScroll, stopScroll } from '../scroll';
import { studioAudio } from '../audio';

export class MaterialDrawer {
  private root: HTMLElement;
  private codeEl: HTMLElement | null;
  private titleEl: HTMLElement | null;
  private discEl: HTMLElement | null;
  private imgEl: HTMLImageElement | null;
  private summaryEl: HTMLElement | null;
  private materialsEl: HTMLElement | null;
  private studyLinkEl: HTMLAnchorElement | null;
  private inquireLinkEl: HTMLAnchorElement | null;
  private closeBtn: HTMLButtonElement | null;
  private backdrop: HTMLElement | null;
  private isOpenState = false;
  private lastFocused: HTMLElement | null = null;
  private onCloseCb: () => void;

  constructor(root: HTMLElement, signal: AbortSignal, onClose: () => void) {
    this.root = root;
    this.onCloseCb = onClose;
    this.codeEl = root.querySelector('[data-sdrawer-code]');
    this.titleEl = root.querySelector('[data-sdrawer-title]');
    this.discEl = root.querySelector('[data-sdrawer-disc]');
    this.imgEl = root.querySelector('[data-sdrawer-img]');
    this.summaryEl = root.querySelector('[data-sdrawer-summary]');
    this.materialsEl = root.querySelector('[data-sdrawer-materials]');
    this.studyLinkEl = root.querySelector('[data-sdrawer-study]');
    this.inquireLinkEl = root.querySelector('[data-sdrawer-inquire]');
    this.closeBtn = root.querySelector('[data-sdrawer-close]');
    this.backdrop = root.querySelector('[data-sdrawer-backdrop]');

    this.closeBtn?.addEventListener('click', () => this.close(), { signal });
    this.backdrop?.addEventListener('click', () => this.close(), { signal });
    window.addEventListener('keydown', this.onKeyDown, { signal });
  }

  public isOpen() {
    return this.isOpenState;
  }

  public openBySlug(slug: string) {
    const study: MaterialStudyNote | undefined = materialStudies.find((s) => s.key === slug);
    if (!study) return;

    this.lastFocused = document.activeElement instanceof HTMLElement ? document.activeElement : null;

    if (this.codeEl) this.codeEl.textContent = study.category;
    if (this.titleEl) this.titleEl.textContent = study.title;
    if (this.discEl) this.discEl.textContent = 'Concept Preview · Material Palette';
    if (this.imgEl) {
      this.imgEl.src = study.image.src;
      this.imgEl.alt = study.alt;
    }
    if (this.summaryEl) this.summaryEl.textContent = study.summary;

    if (this.materialsEl) {
      this.materialsEl.innerHTML = '';
      for (const mat of study.materials) {
        const li = document.createElement('li');
        li.textContent = mat;
        this.materialsEl.appendChild(li);
      }
    }

    if (this.studyLinkEl) {
      this.studyLinkEl.href = '/works/';
      const lbl = this.studyLinkEl.querySelector('span');
      if (lbl) lbl.textContent = 'Explore Completed Works';
    }

    if (this.inquireLinkEl) {
      const msg = `Namaste ${site.founder} ji, I was exploring the Rubiconius 3D Material Study and would like to discuss ${study.title} finishes for my space.`;
      this.inquireLinkEl.href = `https://wa.me/${site.whatsappNumber}?text=${encodeURIComponent(msg)}`;
    }

    this.root.hidden = false;
    requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      this.root.classList.add('is-open');
    });
    this.isOpenState = true;
    stopScroll();
    this.closeBtn?.focus();
  }

  public close() {
    if (!this.isOpenState) return;
    this.isOpenState = false;
    this.root.classList.remove('is-open');
    studioAudio.tick(420, 0.02);
    startScroll();
    this.onCloseCb();

    const finish = () => {
      if (!this.isOpenState) this.root.hidden = true;
    };
    window.setTimeout(finish, 480);
    this.lastFocused?.focus();
  }

  private onKeyDown = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
    if (!this.isOpenState) return;
    if (e.key === 'Escape') {
      e.preventDefault();
      this.close();
      return;
    }
    if (e.key === 'Tab') {
      const focusables = [
        ...this.root.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>(
          'a[href], button:not([disabled]), [tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])'
        ),
      ];
      if (!focusables.length) return;
      const first = focusables[0];
      const last = focusables[focusables.length - 1];
      if (e.shiftKey && document.activeElement === first) {
        e.preventDefault();
        last.focus();
      } else if (!e.shiftKey && document.activeElement === last) {
        e.preventDefault();
        first.focus();
      }
    }
  };
}

--- src\engine\showroom\hints.ts ---

import { isTouch } from '../env';

const STORAGE_KEY = 'rubiconius_showroom_hint_seen';

export class ScrollHint {
  private el: HTMLElement | null;
  private dismissed = false;

  constructor(root: HTMLElement, signal: AbortSignal) {
    this.el = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-showroom-hint]');
    if (!this.el) return;

    this.el.textContent = isTouch() ? 'Swipe to walk' : 'Scroll to walk · Drag to look';

    try {
      if (sessionStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY) === '1') {
        this.dismissed = true;
        this.el.classList.add('is-dismissed');
        return;
      }
    } catch {}

    const dismiss = () => this.dismiss();
    window.addEventListener('wheel', dismiss, { passive: true, once: true, signal });
    window.addEventListener('touchstart', dismiss, { passive: true, once: true, signal });
    window.addEventListener('keydown', dismiss, { passive: true, once: true, signal });
  }

  public dismiss() {
    if (this.dismissed || !this.el) return;
    this.dismissed = true;
    this.el.classList.add('is-dismissed');
    try {
      sessionStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, '1');
    } catch {}
  }
}

--- src\engine\showroom\index.ts ---

import * as THREE from 'three';
import { gsap } from 'gsap';
import { ScrollTrigger } from 'gsap/ScrollTrigger';
import { showroom } from '../../data/site';
import { isTouch, prefersReducedMotion } from '../env';
import { pauseGL, resumeGL } from '../gl';
import { getScrollLimit, onScroll, resizeScroll, scrollState, scrollToTarget } from '../scroll';
import { studioAudio } from '../audio';
import { holdEnter } from '../transition';
import { registerLoaderTask } from '../loader';
import { CameraRail } from './rail';
import { loadShowroomAsset, type LoadedAsset } from './assets';
import { createLivingAtmosphere } from './atmosphere';
import { TactileCamera } from './tactile';
import { SpatialPins } from './pins';
import { MaterialDrawer } from './drawer';
import { ChapterBar } from './chapters';
import { ScrollHint } from './hints';
import { DebugFlyController } from './debug';

export type ShowroomMode = 'fullscreen' | 'inline';

export type ShowroomHandle = {
  ready: Promise<void>;
  dispose: () => void;
};

declare global {
  interface Window {
    __showroomDebug?: {
      getState: () => {
        mode: ShowroomMode;
        u: number;
        targetU: number;
        cam: [number, number, number];
        fov: number;
        dpr: number;
        ready: boolean;
        visible: boolean;
        debugFly: boolean;
        audio: ReturnType<typeof studioAudio.getDebugState>;
      };
      jumpTo: (u: number, immediate?: boolean) => void;
      logWaypoint: () => { cam: number[]; target: number[] } | null;
    };
  }
}

export function mountShowroom(
  root: HTMLElement,
  opts?: { mode?: ShowroomMode }
): ShowroomHandle {
  const mode: ShowroomMode =
    opts?.mode ?? (root.dataset.showroomMode === 'inline' ? 'inline' : 'fullscreen');
  const isInline = mode === 'inline';

  const host = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-showroom-canvas]');
  const drawerEl = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-showroom-drawer]');
  const statusEl = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-showroom-status]');
  const soundBtn = root.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-showroom-sound]');

  if (!host || !drawerEl) {
    return { ready: Promise.resolve(), dispose: () => {} };
  }

  const abort = new AbortController();
  const { signal } = abort;
  let disposed = false;
  let isVisible = !isInline;

  if (!isInline) {
    document.documentElement.classList.add('is-showroom');
    pauseGL();
    studioAudio.startAmbient();
  }

  // Sync sound button (present in fullscreen header) with shared studioAudio state
  const syncSoundBtn = (enabled: boolean) => {
    if (!soundBtn) return;
    soundBtn.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(enabled));
    const lbl = soundBtn.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-sound-label]');
    if (lbl) lbl.textContent = enabled ? 'Sound on' : 'Sound off';
  };
  syncSoundBtn(studioAudio.isEnabled());
  const offAudio = studioAudio.onChange(syncSoundBtn);
  soundBtn?.addEventListener(
    'click',
    () => {
      studioAudio.toggle();
    },
    { signal }
  );

  // Scene & direct WebGLRenderer (native MSAA for GLB, disabled for Spark splats for max FPS)
  const scene = new THREE.Scene();
  scene.background = new THREE.Color(showroom.fog.color);
  scene.fog = new THREE.Fog(showroom.fog.color, showroom.fog.near, showroom.fog.far);

  const w = host.clientWidth || window.innerWidth;
  const h = host.clientHeight || window.innerHeight;
  const resolveFov = (width: number, height: number) =>
    width / Math.max(1, height) < 0.85 ? Math.round(showroom.baseFov * 1.24) : showroom.baseFov;
  const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(resolveFov(w, h), w / h, 0.1, 120);

  const initialDpr = Math.min(window.devicePixelRatio || 1, 1.5);
  const renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({
    antialias: showroom.asset.type !== 'splat',
    powerPreference: 'high-performance',
    alpha: false,
  });
  renderer.setPixelRatio(initialDpr);
  renderer.setSize(w, h, false);
  renderer.outputColorSpace = THREE.SRGBColorSpace;
  renderer.toneMapping =
    showroom.asset.toneMapping === 'aces' ? THREE.ACESFilmicToneMapping : THREE.NoToneMapping;
  renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.0;

  const canvas = renderer.domElement;
  canvas.className = 'showroom-webgl';
  host.appendChild(canvas);

  // Sync Lenis limit to this page's track height immediately
  resizeScroll();

  // Living ICG-style Atmosphere (PMREM studio reflections, swaying leaf shadows, sunbeam motes, shimmering water)
  const atmosphere = createLivingAtmosphere(scene, renderer);

  // Cached layout metrics (avoids forced reflows on every scroll frame)
  let stageW = host.clientWidth || window.innerWidth;
  let stageH = host.clientHeight || window.innerHeight;
  let cachedTrackTop = 0;
  let cachedScrollable = 1;
  const syncMetrics = () => {
    stageW = host.clientWidth || window.innerWidth;
    stageH = host.clientHeight || window.innerHeight;
    cachedTrackTop = window.scrollY + root.getBoundingClientRect().top;
    cachedScrollable = Math.max(1, root.offsetHeight - window.innerHeight);
  };
  syncMetrics();
  ScrollTrigger.addEventListener('refresh', syncMetrics);

  // Inline scroll coordinate helpers
  const computeInlineU = (scrollY = scrollState().y) =>
    THREE.MathUtils.clamp((scrollY - cachedTrackTop) / cachedScrollable, 0, 1);

  const resolveScrollY = (u: number) => {
    const clamped = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(u, 0, 1);
    if (!isInline) {
      return clamped * getScrollLimit();
    }
    return cachedTrackTop + clamped * cachedScrollable;
  };

  // Subsystems
  let rail = new CameraRail(showroom.waypoints);
  const hints = new ScrollHint(root, signal);
  const tactile = new TactileCamera(canvas, showroom.baseFov, signal, () => hints.dismiss());
  const debugFly = new DebugFlyController(camera, canvas, signal);

  const drawer = new MaterialDrawer(drawerEl, signal, () => {
    tactile.setPinTarget(null);
    root.classList.remove('has-drawer-open');
  });

  const chapters = new ChapterBar(
    root,
    showroom.waypoints,
    signal,
    () => {
      hints.dismiss();
      drawer.close();
    },
    isInline ? resolveScrollY : undefined
  );

  const pins = new SpatialPins(root, showroom.waypoints, signal, (wp, anchor) => {
    hints.dismiss();
    tactile.setPinTarget(anchor);
    if (wp.pin) {
      root.classList.add('has-drawer-open');
      drawer.openBySlug(wp.pin.studySlug);
    }
  });

  // Scroll state (reads directly from global Lenis singleton in src/engine/scroll.ts)
  let currentU = isInline ? computeInlineU() : THREE.MathUtils.clamp(scrollState().progress, 0, 1);
  let targetU = currentU;
  let scrollVel = 0;
  let isSnapping = false;
  let snapCooldownUntil = 0;
  let idleFrames = 0;

  const offScroll = onScroll((s) => {
    targetU = isInline ? computeInlineU(s.y) : THREE.MathUtils.clamp(s.progress, 0, 1);
    scrollVel = s.velocity;
    tactile.notifyScrollVelocity(s.velocity);
    if (Math.abs(s.velocity) > 0.25) {
      if (!isInline || isVisible) hints.dismiss();
      idleFrames = 0;
    }
  });

  // Set initial camera frame cleanly
  const railPos = new THREE.Vector3();
  const railLook = new THREE.Vector3();
  rail.samplePosition(currentU, railPos);
  rail.sampleTarget(currentU, railLook);
  camera.position.copy(railPos);
  camera.lookAt(railLook);
  chapters.update(currentU, true);

  // Resize handler
  const onResize = () => {
    if (disposed) return;
    syncMetrics();
    camera.fov = resolveFov(stageW, stageH);
    camera.aspect = stageW / stageH;
    camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
    renderer.setSize(stageW, stageH, false);
  };
  window.addEventListener('resize', onResize, { passive: true, signal });

  // Adaptive DPR monitoring (steps down to 1.5 if sustained frame time > 22ms over 2s)
  let slowAccum = 0;
  let dprSteppedDown = false;
  let lastNow = performance.now();
  const bootTime = lastNow;
  let isReady = false;
  let loadedAsset: LoadedAsset | null = null;

  const renderTick = () => {
    if (disposed || document.hidden) return;
    if (isInline && !isVisible) {
      lastNow = performance.now();
      return;
    }

    const now = performance.now();
    const dt = Math.min(0.05, Math.max(0.001, (now - lastNow) / 1000));
    lastNow = now;
    if (!prefersReducedMotion()) {
      atmosphere.update((now - bootTime) / 1000);
    }

    if (!dprSteppedDown && initialDpr > 1.5 && isReady) {
      if (dt > 0.022) {
        slowAccum += dt;
        if (slowAccum > 2.0) {
          dprSteppedDown = true;
          renderer.setPixelRatio(1.5);
          renderer.setSize(host.clientWidth || window.innerWidth, host.clientHeight || window.innerHeight, false);
        }
      } else {
        slowAccum = Math.max(0, slowAccum - dt * 0.5);
      }
    }

    if (debugFly.isActive()) {
      debugFly.update(dt);
    } else {
      // Smooth damped scrub (~0.6s catch-up matching scrub: 0.6)
      const kScroll = prefersReducedMotion() ? 1 : 1 - Math.exp((-dt / 0.6) * 3.0);
      currentU += (targetU - currentU) * kScroll;

      rail.samplePosition(currentU, railPos);
      rail.sampleTarget(currentU, railLook);
      tactile.apply(camera, railPos, railLook, scrollVel, dt);

      // Gentle ICG-style velocity-settle snap on fullscreen desktop only (never trap inline homepage scroll!)
      if (
        !isInline &&
        !isTouch() &&
        !prefersReducedMotion() &&
        !drawer.isOpen() &&
        !isSnapping &&
        now > snapCooldownUntil
      ) {
        if (Math.abs(scrollVel) < 0.05) {
          idleFrames++;
          if (idleFrames === 12) {
            const limit = getScrollLimit();
            if (limit > 0) {
              const curPx = currentU * limit;
              const hit =
                currentU > 0.985
                  ? rail.waypoints[rail.waypoints.length - 1]
                  : rail.waypoints.find((w) => {
                      const dPx = Math.abs(curPx - w.u * limit);
                      return dPx > 10 && dPx <= 220;
                    });
              if (hit) {
                isSnapping = true;
                scrollToTarget(hit.u * limit, {
                  duration: 0.8,
                  onComplete: () => {
                    isSnapping = false;
                    snapCooldownUntil = performance.now() + 900;
                  },
                });
              }
            }
          }
        } else {
          idleFrames = 0;
        }
      }
    }

    chapters.update(currentU, false);
    pins.update(camera, stageW, stageH, currentU, drawer.isOpen());

    renderer.render(scene, camera);
  };

  gsap.ticker.add(renderTick);

  // Lazy-streamable asset promise
  let loadPromise: Promise<void> | null = null;
  let resolveReady!: () => void;
  const ready = new Promise<void>((r) => {
    resolveReady = r;
  });

  const startLoad = () => {
    if (loadPromise || disposed) return loadPromise ?? ready;
    loadPromise = loadShowroomAsset({
      asset: showroom.asset,
      waypoints: showroom.waypoints,
      scene,
      renderer,
      onProgress: (frac, label) => {
        if (statusEl) {
          statusEl.textContent = `${label} · ${Math.round(frac * 100)}%`;
        }
      },
    })
      .then((loaded) => {
        if (disposed) {
          loaded.dispose();
          return;
        }
        loadedAsset = loaded;
        renderer.toneMapping = loaded.toneMapping;
        atmosphere.attachToLoadedScene(loaded.object3D);

        if (loaded.extractedWaypoints) {
          rail = new CameraRail(loaded.extractedWaypoints);
          pins.updateAnchors(loaded.extractedWaypoints);
          rail.samplePosition(currentU, railPos);
          rail.sampleTarget(currentU, railLook);
          camera.position.copy(railPos);
          camera.lookAt(railLook);
        }

        renderer.render(scene, camera);
        isReady = true;
        root.classList.add('is-ready');
        if (statusEl) statusEl.hidden = true;
        resolveReady();
      })
      .catch((err) => {
        console.error('[Showroom] Failed to load 3D asset:', err);
        if (statusEl) {
          statusEl.textContent = 'Unable to stream 3D scene.';
        }
        isReady = true;
        root.classList.add('is-ready');
        resolveReady();
      });
    return loadPromise;
  };

  let visIo: IntersectionObserver | null = null;
  let loadIo: IntersectionObserver | null = null;

  if (!isInline) {
    startLoad();
    holdEnter(ready, 1800);
    registerLoaderTask(ready);
  } else {
    // Inline mode: hand off GPU cleanly with global OGL and stream when within 900px
    visIo = new IntersectionObserver(
      ([entry]) => {
        if (disposed) return;
        const nowVisible = Boolean(entry?.isIntersecting);
        if (nowVisible === isVisible) return;
        isVisible = nowVisible;
        if (isVisible) {
          pauseGL();
          studioAudio.startAmbient();
          startLoad();
          targetU = computeInlineU();
          currentU = targetU;
          lastNow = performance.now();
          if (isReady) renderer.render(scene, camera);
        } else {
          studioAudio.stopAmbient();
          resumeGL();
        }
      },
      { threshold: 0.01 }
    );
    visIo.observe(root);

    loadIo = new IntersectionObserver(
      ([entry]) => {
        if (disposed) return;
        if (entry?.isIntersecting) {
          startLoad();
          loadIo?.disconnect();
          loadIo = null;
        }
      },
      { rootMargin: '400px 0px' }
    );
    loadIo.observe(root);
  }

  // Read-only telemetry handle for Playwright QA & dev authoring
  window.__showroomDebug = {
    getState: () => ({
      mode,
      u: currentU,
      targetU,
      cam: [
        Math.round(camera.position.x * 1000) / 1000,
        Math.round(camera.position.y * 1000) / 1000,
        Math.round(camera.position.z * 1000) / 1000,
      ],
      fov: Math.round(camera.fov * 100) / 100,
      dpr: renderer.getPixelRatio(),
      ready: isReady,
      visible: isVisible,
      debugFly: debugFly.isActive(),
      audio: studioAudio.getDebugState(),
    }),
    jumpTo: (u: number, immediate = false) => {
      const clamped = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(u, 0, 1);
      if (isInline) startLoad();
      const targetY = resolveScrollY(clamped);
      if (immediate) {
        scrollToTarget(targetY, { immediate: true });
        currentU = clamped;
        targetU = clamped;
        rail.samplePosition(currentU, railPos);
        rail.sampleTarget(currentU, railLook);
        camera.position.copy(railPos);
        camera.lookAt(railLook);
        chapters.update(currentU, true);
        if (isVisible) renderer.render(scene, camera);
      } else {
        scrollToTarget(targetY, { duration: 1.0 });
      }
    },
    logWaypoint: () => (debugFly.isActive() ? debugFly.logWaypoint() : null),
  };

  const dispose = () => {
    if (disposed) return;
    disposed = true;

    visIo?.disconnect();
    loadIo?.disconnect();
    ScrollTrigger.removeEventListener('refresh', syncMetrics);
    gsap.ticker.remove(renderTick);
    abort.abort();
    offScroll();
    offAudio();
    drawer.close();
    studioAudio.stopAmbient();

    if (loadedAsset) {
      loadedAsset.dispose();
      loadedAsset = null;
    }

    atmosphere.dispose();
    renderer.dispose();
    try {
      renderer.forceContextLoss();
    } catch {}
    canvas.remove();

    delete window.__showroomDebug;
    if (!isInline) {
      document.documentElement.classList.remove('is-showroom');
    }
    resumeGL();
  };

  return { ready, dispose };
}

--- src\engine\showroom\pins.ts ---

import * as THREE from 'three';
import type { ShowroomWaypoint } from '../../data/site';
import { studioAudio } from '../audio';

export type ActivePinItem = {
  waypoint: ShowroomWaypoint;
  anchor: THREE.Vector3;
  el: HTMLElement;
  btn: HTMLButtonElement;
};

export class SpatialPins {
  private items: ActivePinItem[] = [];
  private tmp = new THREE.Vector3();

  constructor(
    container: HTMLElement,
    waypoints: ShowroomWaypoint[],
    signal: AbortSignal,
    onSelect: (wp: ShowroomWaypoint, anchor: THREE.Vector3) => void
  ) {
    waypoints.forEach((wp) => {
      if (!wp.pin) return;
      const el = container.querySelector<HTMLElement>(`[data-showroom-pin="${wp.id}"]`);
      const btn = el?.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('button');
      if (!el || !btn) return;

      const anchor = new THREE.Vector3(...wp.pin.anchor);
      const handleClick = () => {
        studioAudio.pin();
        onSelect(wp, anchor);
      };
      btn.addEventListener('click', handleClick, { signal });
      this.items.push({ waypoint: wp, anchor, el, btn });
    });
  }

  public updateAnchors(waypoints: ShowroomWaypoint[]) {
    for (const item of this.items) {
      const updated = waypoints.find((w) => w.id === item.waypoint.id);
      if (updated?.pin) {
        item.waypoint = updated;
        item.anchor.set(...updated.pin.anchor);
      }
    }
  }

  public update(camera: THREE.PerspectiveCamera, width: number, height: number, progressU: number, drawerOpen: boolean) {
    for (const item of this.items) {
      this.tmp.copy(item.anchor).project(camera);

      const isBehind = this.tmp.z > 1.0 || this.tmp.z < -1.0;
      const isOffscreen = Math.abs(this.tmp.x) > 1.08 || Math.abs(this.tmp.y) > 1.08;

      // Smooth fade window around waypoint u so pins arrive organically as you enter each room
      const dist = Math.abs(progressU - item.waypoint.u);
      const visibility = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(1 - (dist - 0.12) / 0.14, 0, 1);

      if (isBehind || isOffscreen || visibility <= 0.02 || drawerOpen) {
        item.el.style.opacity = '0';
        item.el.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
        item.btn.tabIndex = -1;
        item.el.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
      } else {
        const sx = (this.tmp.x * 0.5 + 0.5) * width;
        const sy = (-this.tmp.y * 0.5 + 0.5) * height;
        item.el.classList.toggle('is-left', sx > width - 250);
        item.el.style.transform = `translate3d(${sx.toFixed(1)}px, ${sy.toFixed(1)}px, 0)`;
        item.el.style.opacity = visibility.toFixed(3);
        item.el.style.pointerEvents = visibility > 0.35 ? 'auto' : 'none';
        item.btn.tabIndex = visibility > 0.35 ? 0 : -1;
        item.el.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'false');
      }
    }
  }
}

--- src\engine\showroom\rail.ts ---

import * as THREE from 'three';
import type { ShowroomWaypoint } from '../../data/site';

export type ResolvedWaypoint = ShowroomWaypoint & {
  railU: number;
};

export class CameraRail {
  private posCurve: THREE.CatmullRomCurve3;
  private tgtCurve: THREE.CatmullRomCurve3;
  public waypoints: ResolvedWaypoint[];

  constructor(waypoints: ShowroomWaypoint[]) {
    const camPts = waypoints.map((w) => new THREE.Vector3(...w.cam));
    const tgtPts = waypoints.map((w) => new THREE.Vector3(...w.target));

    this.posCurve = new THREE.CatmullRomCurve3(camPts, false, 'centripetal');
    this.tgtCurve = new THREE.CatmullRomCurve3(tgtPts, false, 'centripetal');

    // Remap each waypoint's canonical u to exact cumulative arc-length along posCurve
    // so clicking chapter N lands squarely on control point N with zero spline drift.
    const segmentLengths = this.computeControlPointU(camPts);
    this.waypoints = waypoints.map((w, idx) => ({
      ...w,
      railU: segmentLengths[idx],
    }));
  }

  private computeControlPointU(pts: THREE.Vector3[]): number[] {
    const n = pts.length;
    if (n <= 1) return [0];

    // Dense sample of posCurve to find closest arc-length parameter for each control point
    const samples = 400;
    const sampled: THREE.Vector3[] = [];
    for (let i = 0; i <= samples; i++) {
      sampled.push(this.posCurve.getPointAt(i / samples));
    }

    const result: number[] = [0];
    let lastBestIdx = 0;
    for (let k = 1; k < n - 1; k++) {
      let bestIdx = lastBestIdx;
      let bestDist = Infinity;
      for (let i = lastBestIdx; i <= samples; i++) {
        const d = sampled[i].distanceToSquared(pts[k]);
        if (d < bestDist) {
          bestDist = d;
          bestIdx = i;
        }
      }
      lastBestIdx = bestIdx;
      result.push(bestIdx / samples);
    }
    result.push(1);
    return result;
  }

  // Maps normalized scroll progress (0..1) through waypoint intervals onto curve arc-length u
  public scrollToCurveU(scrollProgress: number): number {
    const p = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(scrollProgress, 0, 1);
    const wps = this.waypoints;
    if (wps.length <= 1) return p;

    for (let i = 0; i < wps.length - 1; i++) {
      const a = wps[i];
      const b = wps[i + 1];
      if (p >= a.u && p <= b.u) {
        const span = Math.max(0.0001, b.u - a.u);
        const local = (p - a.u) / span;
        return THREE.MathUtils.lerp(a.railU, b.railU, local);
      }
    }
    return p;
  }

  public samplePosition(scrollProgress: number, out: THREE.Vector3): THREE.Vector3 {
    const curveU = this.scrollToCurveU(scrollProgress);
    return this.posCurve.getPointAt(THREE.MathUtils.clamp(curveU, 0, 1), out);
  }

  public sampleTarget(scrollProgress: number, out: THREE.Vector3): THREE.Vector3 {
    const curveU = this.scrollToCurveU(scrollProgress);
    return this.tgtCurve.getPointAt(THREE.MathUtils.clamp(curveU, 0, 1), out);
  }
}

--- src\engine\showroom\tactile.ts ---

import * as THREE from 'three';
import { isTouch, prefersReducedMotion } from '../env';

const DEG = Math.PI / 180;
const PARALLAX_YAW_MAX = 2.5 * DEG;
const PARALLAX_PITCH_MAX = 2.0 * DEG;
const DRAG_YAW_MAX = 12.0 * DEG;
const DRAG_PITCH_MAX = 6.0 * DEG;

export class TactileCamera {
  private pointerTarget = { x: 0, y: 0 };
  private pointer = { x: 0, y: 0 };

  private dragTarget = { yaw: 0, pitch: 0 };
  private drag = { yaw: 0, pitch: 0 };
  private isDragging = false;
  private lastX = 0;
  private lastY = 0;

  private baseFov: number;
  private currentFov: number;

  private pinAnchor: THREE.Vector3 | null = null;
  private pinAimBlend = 0;
  private blendedLook = new THREE.Vector3();
  private hasBlendedLook = false;

  private canvas: HTMLCanvasElement;
  private onInteract?: () => void;

  constructor(
    canvas: HTMLCanvasElement,
    baseFov: number,
    signal: AbortSignal,
    onInteract?: () => void
  ) {
    this.canvas = canvas;
    this.baseFov = baseFov;
    this.currentFov = baseFov;
    this.onInteract = onInteract;

    if (!isTouch() && !prefersReducedMotion()) {
      this.canvas.style.cursor = 'grab';
      window.addEventListener('pointermove', this.onPointerMove, { passive: true, signal });
      this.canvas.addEventListener('pointerdown', this.onPointerDown, { signal });
      window.addEventListener('pointerup', this.onPointerUp, { passive: true, signal });
      window.addEventListener('pointercancel', this.onPointerUp, { passive: true, signal });
      window.addEventListener('blur', this.onBlur, { passive: true, signal });
    }
  }

  private onPointerMove = (e: PointerEvent) => {
    const nx = (e.clientX / Math.max(1, innerWidth)) * 2 - 1;
    const ny = (e.clientY / Math.max(1, innerHeight)) * 2 - 1;
    this.pointerTarget.x = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(nx, -1, 1);
    this.pointerTarget.y = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(ny, -1, 1);

    if (this.isDragging) {
      const dx = e.clientX - this.lastX;
      const dy = e.clientY - this.lastY;
