      this.lastX = e.clientX;
      this.lastY = e.clientY;
      const sens = 0.0032;
      this.dragTarget.yaw = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(this.dragTarget.yaw - dx * sens, -DRAG_YAW_MAX, DRAG_YAW_MAX);
      this.dragTarget.pitch = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(this.dragTarget.pitch - dy * sens, -DRAG_PITCH_MAX, DRAG_PITCH_MAX);
      if (Math.abs(dx) + Math.abs(dy) > 2) this.onInteract?.();
    }
  };

  private onPointerDown = (e: PointerEvent) => {
    if (e.button !== 0) return;
    this.isDragging = true;
    this.lastX = e.clientX;
    this.lastY = e.clientY;
    this.canvas.style.cursor = 'grabbing';
  };

  private onPointerUp = () => {
    if (!this.isDragging) return;
    this.isDragging = false;
    this.canvas.style.cursor = 'grab';
  };

  private onBlur = () => {
    this.isDragging = false;
    this.pointerTarget.x = 0;
    this.pointerTarget.y = 0;
    this.canvas.style.cursor = 'grab';
  };

  public setPinTarget(anchor: THREE.Vector3 | null) {
    this.pinAnchor = anchor ? anchor.clone() : null;
  }

  public notifyScrollVelocity(velocity: number) {
    // Gently recenter click-and-drag look offset when user resumes scrolling
    if (!this.isDragging && Math.abs(velocity) > 0.35) {
      this.dragTarget.yaw *= 0.88;
      this.dragTarget.pitch *= 0.88;
    }
  }

  public apply(
    camera: THREE.PerspectiveCamera,
    railPos: THREE.Vector3,
    railLook: THREE.Vector3,
    scrollVelocity: number,
    dt: number
  ) {
    const reduced = prefersReducedMotion();
    const kPointer = 1 - Math.exp(-5.5 * dt);
    const kDrag = 1 - Math.exp(-7.5 * dt);
    const kRecenter = 1 - Math.exp(-2.8 * dt);

    // Smooth pointer parallax
    this.pointer.x += (this.pointerTarget.x - this.pointer.x) * kPointer;
    this.pointer.y += (this.pointerTarget.y - this.pointer.y) * kPointer;

    // Gentle recentering of drag-to-look after release
    if (!this.isDragging && !this.pinAnchor) {
      this.dragTarget.yaw += (0 - this.dragTarget.yaw) * kRecenter * 0.45;
      this.dragTarget.pitch += (0 - this.dragTarget.pitch) * kRecenter * 0.45;
    }
    this.drag.yaw += (this.dragTarget.yaw - this.drag.yaw) * kDrag;
    this.drag.pitch += (this.dragTarget.pitch - this.drag.pitch) * kDrag;

    // Pin camera aim easing (gently lean lookAt 35% toward pin.anchor while drawer is open)
    const targetPinBlend = this.pinAnchor ? 0.38 : 0;
    this.pinAimBlend += (targetPinBlend - this.pinAimBlend) * (1 - Math.exp(-5.0 * dt));

    const desiredLook = railLook.clone();
    if (this.pinAnchor && this.pinAimBlend > 0.002) {
      desiredLook.lerp(this.pinAnchor, this.pinAimBlend);
    }

    if (!this.hasBlendedLook) {
      this.blendedLook.copy(desiredLook);
      this.hasBlendedLook = true;
    } else {
      this.blendedLook.lerp(desiredLook, 1 - Math.exp(-10.0 * dt));
    }

    camera.position.copy(railPos);
    camera.lookAt(this.blendedLook);

    // Apply damped pointer parallax + click-and-drag-to-look rotations on desktop
    if (!isTouch() && !reduced) {
      const yaw = -this.pointer.x * PARALLAX_YAW_MAX + this.drag.yaw;
      const pitch = -this.pointer.y * PARALLAX_PITCH_MAX + this.drag.pitch;
      camera.rotateY(yaw);
      camera.rotateX(pitch);
    }

    // Subtle FOV breathing: +2° during faster scroll glides, settling smoothly back at rest
    if (!reduced) {
      const speedNorm = THREE.MathUtils.clamp(Math.abs(scrollVelocity) / 28, 0, 1);
      const targetFov = this.baseFov + speedNorm * 2.0;
      const kFov = 1 - Math.exp(-4.5 * dt);
      const nextFov = this.currentFov + (targetFov - this.currentFov) * kFov;
      if (Math.abs(nextFov - this.currentFov) > 0.005) {
        this.currentFov = nextFov;
        camera.fov = this.currentFov;
        camera.updateProjectionMatrix();
      }
    }
  }
}

--- src\engine\astro.ts ---

import { boot, cleanup, enter, leave, syncFlags } from './index';

let navigating = false;

export function start(setup?: () => void) {
  document.addEventListener('astro:before-preparation', (e) => {
    navigating = true;
    const load = e.loader;
    e.loader = async () => { await Promise.all([leave(), load()]); };
  });
  document.addEventListener('astro:before-swap', () => cleanup());
  document.addEventListener('astro:after-swap', () => {
    syncFlags();
    document.getElementById('loader')?.remove();
  });
  document.addEventListener('astro:page-load', () => {
    if (!navigating) return void boot(setup);
    navigating = false;
    enter();
  });
  boot(setup);
}

--- src\engine\audio.ts ---

// Zero-byte Web Audio API studio acoustics & micro-haptics synthesizer. Opt-in only, unlocked on user gesture.
import { prefersReducedMotion } from './env';

type AmbientNodes = {
  src: AudioBufferSourceNode;
  lfo: OscillatorNode;
  lfoGain: GainNode;
  master: GainNode;
};

class StudioAudio {
  private ctx: AudioContext | null = null;
  private enabled = false;
  private ambientWanted = false;
  private ambient: AmbientNodes | null = null;
  private noiseBuffer: AudioBuffer | null = null;
  private listeners = new Set<(enabled: boolean) => void>();

  isEnabled() {
    return this.enabled && !prefersReducedMotion();
  }

  onChange(fn: (enabled: boolean) => void) {
    this.listeners.add(fn);
    return () => {
      this.listeners.delete(fn);
    };
  }

  private emit() {
    const state = this.isEnabled();
    this.listeners.forEach((fn) => fn(state));
  }

  private ensureContext(): AudioContext | null {
    const Ctx =
      window.AudioContext ||
      (window as unknown as { webkitAudioContext: typeof AudioContext }).webkitAudioContext;
    if (!Ctx) return null;
    if (!this.ctx) this.ctx = new Ctx();
    if (this.ctx.state === 'suspended') {
      this.ctx.resume().catch(() => {});
    }
    return this.ctx;
  }

  private ensureBrownNoise(ctx: AudioContext): AudioBuffer {
    if (this.noiseBuffer && this.noiseBuffer.sampleRate === ctx.sampleRate) {
      return this.noiseBuffer;
    }
    const len = ctx.sampleRate * 4;
    const buffer = ctx.createBuffer(1, len, ctx.sampleRate);
    const data = buffer.getChannelData(0);
    let last = 0;
    for (let i = 0; i < len; i++) {
      const white = Math.random() * 2 - 1;
      last = (last + 0.02 * white) / 1.02;
      data[i] = last * 3.5;
    }
    this.noiseBuffer = buffer;
    return buffer;
  }

  toggle(): boolean {
    if (prefersReducedMotion()) {
      this.enabled = false;
      this.teardownAmbient(true);
      this.emit();
      return false;
    }
    if (!this.enabled) {
      this.ensureContext();
      this.enabled = true;
      this.tick(640, 0.045);
      if (this.ambientWanted) this.bootAmbient();
    } else {
      this.enabled = false;
      this.teardownAmbient(false);
    }
    this.emit();
    return this.enabled;
  }

  tick(freq = 520, gainPeak = 0.035) {
    if (!this.enabled || !this.ctx || prefersReducedMotion()) return;
    const now = this.ctx.currentTime;
    const osc = this.ctx.createOscillator();
    const gain = this.ctx.createGain();
    osc.type = 'sine';
    osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, now);
    osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(freq * 0.68, now + 0.045);
    gain.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, now);
    gain.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(gainPeak, now + 0.006);
    gain.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, now + 0.055);
    osc.connect(gain);
    gain.connect(this.ctx.destination);
    osc.start(now);
    osc.stop(now + 0.06);
  }

  // Soft tactile architectural pin click: warm sine ping + 2ms crisp highpass impulse
  pin() {
    if (!this.enabled || !this.ctx || prefersReducedMotion()) return;
    this.tick(880, 0.03);
    const ctx = this.ctx;
    const now = ctx.currentTime;
    const len = Math.max(1, Math.floor(ctx.sampleRate * 0.0025));
    const buf = ctx.createBuffer(1, len, ctx.sampleRate);
    const ch = buf.getChannelData(0);
    for (let i = 0; i < len; i++) {
      ch[i] = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * (1 - i / len);
    }
    const src = ctx.createBufferSource();
    src.buffer = buf;
    const hp = ctx.createBiquadFilter();
    hp.type = 'highpass';
    hp.frequency.value = 1900;
    const g = ctx.createGain();
    g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.028, now);
    g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, now + 0.012);
    src.connect(hp);
    hp.connect(g);
    g.connect(ctx.destination);
    src.start(now);
    src.stop(now + 0.015);
  }

  // Subtle architectural room tone: brown noise -> lowpass 220Hz -> peaking 120Hz + 0.07Hz LFO
  startAmbient() {
    this.ambientWanted = true;
    if (!this.enabled || prefersReducedMotion()) return;
    this.bootAmbient();
  }

  stopAmbient() {
    this.ambientWanted = false;
    this.teardownAmbient(false);
  }

  private bootAmbient() {
    if (this.ambient || prefersReducedMotion()) return;
    const ctx = this.ensureContext();
    if (!ctx) return;
    const now = ctx.currentTime;

    const src = ctx.createBufferSource();
    src.buffer = this.ensureBrownNoise(ctx);
    src.loop = true;

    const lowpass = ctx.createBiquadFilter();
    lowpass.type = 'lowpass';
    lowpass.frequency.value = 220;
    lowpass.Q.value = 0.7;

    const body = ctx.createBiquadFilter();
    body.type = 'peaking';
    body.frequency.value = 120;
    body.Q.value = 1.4;
    body.gain.value = 4.5;

    const master = ctx.createGain();
    master.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, now);
    master.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.012, now + 1.2);

    const lfo = ctx.createOscillator();
    lfo.type = 'sine';
    lfo.frequency.value = 0.07;
    const lfoGain = ctx.createGain();
    lfoGain.gain.value = 0.003;
    lfo.connect(lfoGain);
    lfoGain.connect(master.gain);

    src.connect(lowpass);
    lowpass.connect(body);
    body.connect(master);
    master.connect(ctx.destination);

    src.start(now);
    lfo.start(now);
    this.ambient = { src, lfo, lfoGain, master };
  }

  private teardownAmbient(immediate: boolean) {
    const amb = this.ambient;
    if (!amb) return;
    this.ambient = null;
    const ctx = this.ctx;
    if (!ctx || immediate) {
      try { amb.src.stop(); } catch {}
      try { amb.lfo.stop(); } catch {}
      try { amb.master.disconnect(); } catch {}
      return;
    }
    const now = ctx.currentTime;
    try {
      amb.master.gain.cancelScheduledValues(now);
      amb.master.gain.setValueAtTime(Math.max(0.0001, amb.master.gain.value), now);
      amb.master.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, now + 0.6);
      setTimeout(() => {
        try { amb.src.stop(); } catch {}
        try { amb.lfo.stop(); } catch {}
        try { amb.master.disconnect(); } catch {}
      }, 650);
    } catch {}
  }

  // Diagnostic state for automated QA checks
  getDebugState() {
    return {
      enabled: this.enabled,
      contextState: this.ctx?.state ?? 'uninitialized',
      ambientActive: this.ambient !== null,
      ambientWanted: this.ambientWanted,
    };
  }
}

export const studioAudio = new StudioAudio();

--- src\engine\env.ts ---

export const prefersReducedMotion = () => matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches;
export const isTouch = () => matchMedia('(hover: none), (pointer: coarse)').matches;
export const isMobile = () => matchMedia('(max-width: 767px)').matches;

export const ease = {
  out: 'expo.out',
  inOut: 'expo.inOut',
  soft: 'power3.out',
  spring: 'back.out(1.6)',
} as const;

--- src\engine\index.ts ---

import { gsap } from 'gsap';
import { initScroll, resetScroll, resizeScroll, startScroll, stopScroll } from './scroll';
import { initReveals } from './reveal';
import { initGL, mountGL, unmountGL } from './gl';
import { initCursor, initMagnetic } from './interactions';
import { runLoader } from './loader';
import { playEnter, playLeave } from './transition';
import { prefersReducedMotion } from './env';
import { ScrollTrigger } from 'gsap/ScrollTrigger';

export { onScroll, scrollState, scrollToTarget, getScrollLimit, resizeScroll, stopScroll, startScroll } from './scroll';
export { setTransition, holdEnter } from './transition';
export { setLoaderFormat, setLoaderExit, registerLoaderTask } from './loader';
export { hideGL, pauseGL, resumeGL, setBackground, clearBackground, glSupported } from './gl';
export { ease, isTouch, isMobile, prefersReducedMotion } from './env';
export { studioAudio } from './audio';

export type PageFn = (page: { intro: gsap.core.Timeline; path: string }) => void | (() => void);
const pageFns = new Set<PageFn>();
const cleanups: (() => void)[] = [];
let ctx: gsap.Context | null = null;
let intro: gsap.core.Timeline | null = null;
let ready = false;
let booted = false;

export function onPage(fn: PageFn) {
  pageFns.add(fn);
  if (ready) runPageFn(fn);
  return () => { pageFns.delete(fn); };
}

function runPageFn(fn: PageFn) {
  ctx?.add(() => {
    const c = fn({ intro: intro!, path: location.pathname });
    if (typeof c === 'function') cleanups.push(c);
  });
}

export function syncFlags() {
  const h = document.documentElement.classList;
  h.add('js', 'is-booted');
  h.toggle('motion', !prefersReducedMotion());
  if (document.querySelector('.gl-canvas')) h.add('gl-on');
}

function initPage() {
  syncFlags();
  document.getElementById('cursor')?.classList.remove('is-active');
  ctx = gsap.context(() => {
    intro = initReveals(document);
    initMagnetic(document);
  });
  mountGL(document);
  ready = true;
  pageFns.forEach(runPageFn);
  requestAnimationFrame(() => {
    resizeScroll();
    if (ScrollTrigger.getAll().length > 0) ScrollTrigger.refresh();
  });
}

export async function boot(setup?: () => void) {
  if (booted) return;
  booted = true;
  syncFlags();
  initScroll();
  if (document.querySelector('[data-gl]')) initGL();
  initCursor();
  setup?.();
  stopScroll();
  initPage();
  await runLoader();
  startScroll();
  intro?.play();
}

export async function leave() {
  stopScroll();
  await playLeave();
}

export function cleanup() {
  if (!ready) return;
  ready = false;
  cleanups.splice(0).forEach((c) => c());
  ctx?.revert();
  ctx = null;
  unmountGL();
}

export async function enter({ transition = true, scrollTop = true } = {}) {
  if (!booted) return;
  cleanup();
  if (scrollTop) resetScroll();
  initPage();
  if (transition) await playEnter();
  startScroll();
  intro?.play();
}

--- src\engine\interactions.ts ---

import { gsap } from 'gsap';
import { isTouch, prefersReducedMotion } from './env';
import { onScroll } from './scroll';

export function initMagnetic(root: ParentNode = document) {
  if (isTouch() || prefersReducedMotion()) return;
  root.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[data-magnetic]').forEach((el) => {
    const strength = parseFloat(el.dataset.magnetic || '0.3');
    const x = gsap.quickTo(el, 'x', { duration: 0.55, ease: 'power3.out' });
    const y = gsap.quickTo(el, 'y', { duration: 0.55, ease: 'power3.out' });
    const move = (e: PointerEvent) => {
      const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
      x((e.clientX - (r.left + r.width / 2)) * strength);
      y((e.clientY - (r.top + r.height / 2)) * strength);
    };
    const leave = () => { x(0); y(0); };
    el.addEventListener('pointermove', move);
    el.addEventListener('pointerleave', leave);
  });
}

let follower: HTMLElement | null = null;
let px = -999, py = -999;

function syncCursorTarget(xPos: number, yPos: number) {
  if (!follower) return;
  const label = follower.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-cursor-label]');
  const hit = xPos >= 0 && yPos >= 0 ? document.elementFromPoint(xPos, yPos) : null;
  const target = (hit as HTMLElement | null)?.closest<HTMLElement>('[data-cursor]');
  const text = target?.dataset.cursor || '';
  follower.classList.toggle('is-active', !!text);
  if (label && label.textContent !== text) label.textContent = text;
}

export function initCursor() {
  if (follower || isTouch() || prefersReducedMotion()) return;
  follower = document.getElementById('cursor');
  if (!follower) return;
  const x = gsap.quickTo(follower, 'x', { duration: 0.42, ease: 'power3.out' });
  const y = gsap.quickTo(follower, 'y', { duration: 0.42, ease: 'power3.out' });
  let scrollTimer: number | null = null;
  addEventListener('pointermove', (e) => {
    px = e.clientX;
    py = e.clientY;
    x(px); y(py);
    syncCursorTarget(px, py);
  }, { passive: true });
  // Avoid forced elementFromPoint layout flushes during active scroll; resync once scroll settles
  onScroll(() => {
    follower?.classList.remove('is-active');
    if (scrollTimer !== null) window.clearTimeout(scrollTimer);
    scrollTimer = window.setTimeout(() => syncCursorTarget(px, py), 180);
  });
  document.addEventListener('pointerleave', () => follower?.classList.remove('is-active'));
  follower.classList.add('is-ready');
}

--- src\engine\loader.ts ---

import { gsap } from 'gsap';
import { ease, prefersReducedMotion } from './env';

let format = (p: number) => String(Math.round(p)).padStart(3, '0');
export const setLoaderFormat = (fn: (p: number) => string) => { format = fn; };
let exit: ((root: HTMLElement) => gsap.core.Timeline) | null = null;
export const setLoaderExit = (fn: (root: HTMLElement) => gsap.core.Timeline) => { exit = fn; };

const MIN = 0.18;
const MAX = 0.45;
const extraTasks: Promise<unknown>[] = [];

export function registerLoaderTask(p: Promise<unknown>) {
  extraTasks.push(p.catch(() => {}));
}

function criticalAssets(): Promise<unknown>[] {
  const imgs = [...document.querySelectorAll<HTMLImageElement>('img[data-critical], [data-intro] img, [data-gl-instant] img')];
  const tasks = extraTasks.splice(0);
  return [document.fonts.ready, ...tasks, ...imgs.map((i) => (i.complete ? Promise.resolve() : i.decode().catch(() => {})))];
}

export async function runLoader(): Promise<void> {
  const root = document.getElementById('loader');
  if (!root) return;
  if (prefersReducedMotion()) {
    finish(root);
    return;
  }
  const count = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-loader-count]');
  const bar = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-loader-bar]');
  const navElapsed = performance.now() / 1000;
  const slowBoot = navElapsed > 1.05;
  const minWait = slowBoot ? 0.08 : MIN;
  const maxWait = slowBoot ? 0.18 : MAX;
  let assetsReady = false;
  Promise.race([Promise.all(criticalAssets()), wait(maxWait)]).then(() => { assetsReady = true; });
  const start = performance.now();

  await new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
    const tick = () => {
      const elapsed = (performance.now() - start) / 1000;
      const linear = Math.min(100, (elapsed / Math.max(0.05, minWait)) * 100);
      const pct = assetsReady && elapsed >= minWait ? 100 : elapsed >= maxWait ? 100 : Math.min(92, linear);
      if (count) count.textContent = format(pct);
      if (bar) bar.style.transform = `scaleX(${pct / 100})`;
      if (pct >= 100) { gsap.ticker.remove(tick); resolve(); }
    };
    gsap.ticker.add(tick);
  });

  if (exit) {
    const tl = exit(root);
    if (slowBoot) tl.timeScale(1.85);
    await tl;
    return finish(root);
  }
  await gsap.timeline()
    .to(root.querySelectorAll('[data-loader-out]'), { yPercent: -110, duration: 0.35, ease: ease.inOut })
    .to(root, { autoAlpha: 0, duration: 0.4, ease: ease.inOut }, '-=0.15');
  finish(root);
}

function finish(root: HTMLElement) {
  root.hidden = true;
  document.documentElement.classList.add('is-loaded');
}

const wait = (s: number) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, s * 1000));

--- src\engine\reveal.ts ---

import { gsap } from 'gsap';
import { ScrollTrigger } from 'gsap/ScrollTrigger';
import { SplitText } from 'gsap/SplitText';
import { ease, prefersReducedMotion } from './env';

gsap.registerPlugin(ScrollTrigger, SplitText);

const durations = { lines: 1.2, words: 1.1, chars: 0.9 } as const;
const staggers = { lines: 0.08, words: 0.035, chars: 0.018 } as const;

function splitTween(el: HTMLElement, isIntro: boolean) {
  const type = (el.dataset.split || 'lines') as keyof typeof durations;
  const delay = parseFloat(el.dataset.delay || '0');
  const s = { tween: undefined as gsap.core.Tween | undefined, done: false, requested: false };
  SplitText.create(el, {
    type: type === 'lines' ? 'lines' : `lines,${type}`,
    mask: 'lines',
    autoSplit: true,
    linesClass: 'split-line',
    onSplit(self) {
      gsap.set(el, { visibility: 'visible' });
      if (s.done) return;
      const targets = type === 'lines' ? self.lines : type === 'words' ? self.words : self.chars;
      s.tween = gsap.from(targets, {
        yPercent: 110,
        rotate: type === 'chars' ? 0 : 1.5,
        duration: durations[type],
        stagger: staggers[type],
        ease: ease.out,
        delay,
        paused: isIntro && !s.requested,
        onComplete: () => {
          s.done = true;
          try { self.revert(); } catch {}
        },
        scrollTrigger: isIntro ? undefined : { trigger: el, start: 'top 88%', once: true },
      });
      return s.tween;
    },
  });
  return () => { s.requested = true; s.tween?.play(); };
}

function blockTween(el: HTMLElement, paused: boolean) {
  const kind = el.dataset.reveal || 'up';
  const delay = parseFloat(el.dataset.delay || '0');
  const from: gsap.TweenVars =
    kind === 'fade' ? { autoAlpha: 0 }
    : kind === 'clip' ? { clipPath: 'inset(100% 0% 0% 0%)', autoAlpha: 1 }
    : kind === 'scale' ? { scale: 1.12, clipPath: 'inset(10% 10% 10% 10%)', autoAlpha: 1 }
    : { y: 40, autoAlpha: 0 };
  const to: gsap.TweenVars = kind === 'clip' || kind === 'scale' ? { clipPath: 'inset(0% 0% 0% 0%)', scale: 1 } : {};
  gsap.set(el, { visibility: 'visible' });
  return gsap.fromTo(el, from, {
    ...to,
    y: 0,
    autoAlpha: 1,
    duration: kind === 'fade' ? 0.95 : 1.2,
    ease: ease.out,
    delay,
    paused,
    scrollTrigger: paused ? undefined : { trigger: el, start: 'top 90%', once: true },
  });
}

export function initReveals(root: ParentNode = document) {
  const intro = gsap.timeline({ paused: true });
  if (prefersReducedMotion()) return intro;

  root.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[data-split]').forEach((el) => {
    const isIntro = el.hasAttribute('data-intro');
    const play = splitTween(el, isIntro);
    if (isIntro) intro.add(play, 0);
  });
  root.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[data-reveal]').forEach((el) => {
    const isIntro = el.hasAttribute('data-intro');
    const t = blockTween(el, isIntro);
    if (isIntro) intro.add(() => t.play(), 0);
  });
  root.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[data-parallax]').forEach((el) => {
    const amount = parseFloat(el.dataset.parallax || '0.12');
    gsap.fromTo(el, { yPercent: -amount * 100 }, {
      yPercent: amount * 100,
      ease: 'none',
      scrollTrigger: { trigger: el.parentElement || el, start: 'top bottom', end: 'bottom top', scrub: 0.6 },
    });
  });
  return intro;
}

--- src\engine\scroll.ts ---

import Lenis from 'lenis';
import { gsap } from 'gsap';
import { ScrollTrigger } from 'gsap/ScrollTrigger';
import { prefersReducedMotion } from './env';

gsap.registerPlugin(ScrollTrigger);

let lenis: Lenis | null = null;
const listeners = new Set<(s: ScrollState) => void>();

export type ScrollState = { y: number; velocity: number; progress: number };
const state: ScrollState = { y: 0, velocity: 0, progress: 0 };

export function initScroll() {
  if (lenis || prefersReducedMotion()) {
    if (!lenis) addEventListener('scroll', nativeTick, { passive: true });
    return;
  }
  lenis = new Lenis({
    duration: 1.2,
    easing: (t) => Math.min(1, 1.001 - Math.pow(2, -10 * t)),
    smoothWheel: true,
    wheelMultiplier: 1.0,
    touchMultiplier: 1.2,
    anchors: true,
  });
  lenis.on('scroll', (l: Lenis) => {
    state.y = l.scroll;
    state.velocity = l.velocity;
    state.progress = l.limit > 0 ? l.scroll / l.limit : 0;
    if (ScrollTrigger.getAll().length > 0) ScrollTrigger.update();
    listeners.forEach((fn) => fn(state));
  });
  gsap.ticker.add((time) => lenis?.raf(time * 1000));
  gsap.ticker.lagSmoothing(500, 33);
}

function nativeTick() {
  const max = document.documentElement.scrollHeight - innerHeight;
  state.velocity = scrollY - state.y;
  state.y = scrollY;
  state.progress = max > 0 ? scrollY / max : 0;
  listeners.forEach((fn) => fn(state));
}

export const onScroll = (fn: (s: ScrollState) => void) => {
  listeners.add(fn);
  return () => listeners.delete(fn);
};
export const scrollState = () => state;
export const stopScroll = () => lenis?.stop();
export const startScroll = () => lenis?.start();
export function resetScroll() {
  if (lenis) lenis.scrollTo(0, { immediate: true, force: true });
  else scrollTo(0, 0);
  state.y = 0;
  state.velocity = 0;
  state.progress = 0;
}
export function resizeScroll() {
  lenis?.resize();
}
export function getScrollLimit(): number {
  lenis?.resize();
  if (lenis && lenis.limit > 0) return lenis.limit;
  return Math.max(0, document.documentElement.scrollHeight - innerHeight);
}
export function scrollToTarget(
  target: string | HTMLElement | number,
  opts?: { duration?: number; immediate?: boolean; onComplete?: () => void }
) {
  if (lenis) {
    lenis.scrollTo(target, {
      duration: opts?.immediate ? 0 : (opts?.duration ?? 1.2),
      immediate: opts?.immediate ?? false,
      easing: (t) => Math.min(1, 1.001 - Math.pow(2, -10 * t)),
      onComplete: opts?.onComplete,
    });
  } else if (typeof target === 'number') {
    scrollTo({ top: target, behavior: opts?.immediate ? 'auto' : 'smooth' });
    opts?.onComplete?.();
  } else {
    (typeof target === 'string' ? document.querySelector(target) : target)?.scrollIntoView();
    opts?.onComplete?.();
  }
}

--- src\engine\transition.ts ---

import { gsap } from 'gsap';
import { ease, prefersReducedMotion } from './env';

export type Transition = {
  leave: (curtain: HTMLElement) => Promise<unknown> | gsap.core.Animation;
  enter: (curtain: HTMLElement) => Promise<unknown> | gsap.core.Animation;
};

let current: Transition = {
  leave: (c) => gsap.timeline()
    .set(c, { autoAlpha: 1 })
    .fromTo(c.querySelector('.curtain__panel'), { clipPath: 'inset(100% 0% 0% 0%)' }, { clipPath: 'inset(0% 0% 0% 0%)', duration: 0.72, ease: ease.inOut }),
  enter: (c) => gsap.timeline()
    .to(c.querySelector('.curtain__panel'), { clipPath: 'inset(0% 0% 100% 0%)', duration: 0.78, ease: ease.inOut })
    .set(c, { autoAlpha: 0 }),
};

const reduced: Transition = {
  leave: (c) => gsap.timeline().set(c, { autoAlpha: 1 }).fromTo(c, { opacity: 0 }, { opacity: 1, duration: 0.2 }),
  enter: (c) => gsap.to(c, { autoAlpha: 0, duration: 0.2 }),
};

export const setTransition = (t: Transition) => { current = t; };
const pick = () => (prefersReducedMotion() ? reduced : current);
const curtain = () => document.getElementById('curtain') as HTMLElement;

let pendingHold: Promise<unknown> | null = null;

export function holdEnter(p: Promise<unknown>, maxMs = 1500) {
  const cap = new Promise<void>((r) => setTimeout(r, maxMs));
  pendingHold = Promise.race([p.catch(() => {}), cap]);
}

export const playLeave = () => Promise.resolve(pick().leave(curtain()));
export const playEnter = async () => {
  if (pendingHold) {
    const hold = pendingHold;
    pendingHold = null;
    await hold;
  }
  return Promise.resolve(pick().enter(curtain()));
};

--- src\motion\heroVideoMachine.ts ---

export type HeroVideoState =
  | 'poster'
  | 'playing'
  | 'pausedByUser'
  | 'pausedOffscreen'
  | 'reducedMotion';

export type HeroVideoEvent =
  | 'CAN_PLAY'
  | 'USER_TOGGLE'
  | 'OUT_OF_VIEW'
  | 'IN_VIEW'
  | 'PLAY_FAILED';

export interface HeroVideoContext {
  inView: boolean;
  canPlay: boolean;
  userPaused: boolean;
  autoplayBlocked: boolean;
  reducedMotion: boolean;
}

export interface HeroVideoSnapshot {
  state: HeroVideoState;
  context: HeroVideoContext;
}

export interface HeroVideoOptions {
  reducedMotion?: boolean;
  inView?: boolean;
  canPlay?: boolean;
}

export type HeroVideoListener = (snapshot: HeroVideoSnapshot) => void;

const canAutoResume = (ctx: HeroVideoContext): boolean =>
  !ctx.reducedMotion && ctx.canPlay && ctx.inView && !ctx.userPaused && !ctx.autoplayBlocked;

/**
 * Pure deterministic finite state transition for hero video playback.
 * Guards ensure:
 * - No event ever exits `reducedMotion`
 * - `IN_VIEW` never exits `pausedByUser`
 * - `OUT_OF_VIEW` pauses active playback to `pausedOffscreen`
 */
export function transitionHeroVideo(
  snapshot: HeroVideoSnapshot,
  event: HeroVideoEvent
): HeroVideoSnapshot {
  const { state, context } = snapshot;

  // Hard guard: reducedMotion is an absorbing terminal state.
  if (state === 'reducedMotion' || context.reducedMotion) {
    return state === 'reducedMotion'
      ? snapshot
      : { state: 'reducedMotion', context: { ...context, reducedMotion: true } };
  }

  switch (event) {
    case 'CAN_PLAY': {
      const nextCtx: HeroVideoContext = { ...context, canPlay: true };
      if (state === 'pausedByUser' || nextCtx.userPaused) {
        return { state: 'pausedByUser', context: nextCtx };
      }
      if (!nextCtx.inView) {
        return { state: 'pausedOffscreen', context: nextCtx };
      }
      if (nextCtx.autoplayBlocked) {
        return { state: 'poster', context: nextCtx };
      }
      return { state: 'playing', context: nextCtx };
    }

    case 'USER_TOGGLE': {
      // If browser blocked initial autoplay, a manual toggle acts as an explicit Play gesture.
      if (context.autoplayBlocked) {
        const nextCtx: HeroVideoContext = {
          ...context,
          autoplayBlocked: false,
          userPaused: false,
        };
        if (nextCtx.canPlay && nextCtx.inView) {
          return { state: 'playing', context: nextCtx };
        }
        return {
          state: nextCtx.canPlay ? 'pausedOffscreen' : 'poster',
          context: nextCtx,
        };
      }

      if (state === 'pausedByUser' || context.userPaused) {
        const nextCtx: HeroVideoContext = { ...context, userPaused: false };
        if (nextCtx.canPlay && nextCtx.inView) {
          return { state: 'playing', context: nextCtx };
        }
        if (nextCtx.canPlay && !nextCtx.inView) {
          return { state: 'pausedOffscreen', context: nextCtx };
        }
        return { state: 'poster', context: nextCtx };
      }

      const nextCtx: HeroVideoContext = { ...context, userPaused: true };
      return { state: 'pausedByUser', context: nextCtx };
    }

    case 'OUT_OF_VIEW': {
      const nextCtx: HeroVideoContext = { ...context, inView: false };
      if (state === 'pausedByUser') {
        return { state: 'pausedByUser', context: nextCtx };
      }
      if (!nextCtx.autoplayBlocked && (state === 'playing' || nextCtx.canPlay)) {
        return { state: 'pausedOffscreen', context: nextCtx };
      }
      return { state: 'poster', context: nextCtx };
    }

    case 'IN_VIEW': {
      const nextCtx: HeroVideoContext = { ...context, inView: true };
      // Guard: IN_VIEW must never exit pausedByUser.
      if (state === 'pausedByUser' || nextCtx.userPaused) {
        return { state: 'pausedByUser', context: nextCtx };
      }
      if (canAutoResume(nextCtx)) {
        return { state: 'playing', context: nextCtx };
      }
      return { state: 'poster', context: nextCtx };
    }

    case 'PLAY_FAILED': {
      const nextCtx: HeroVideoContext = { ...context, autoplayBlocked: true };
      if (state === 'pausedByUser') {
        return { state: 'pausedByUser', context: nextCtx };
      }
      return { state: 'poster', context: nextCtx };
    }
  }
}

export function createHeroVideoMachine(options: HeroVideoOptions = {}) {
  const reducedMotion = Boolean(options.reducedMotion);
  const inView = options.inView ?? true;
  const canPlay = options.canPlay ?? false;

  let snapshot: HeroVideoSnapshot = {
    state: reducedMotion ? 'reducedMotion' : canPlay && inView ? 'playing' : 'poster',
    context: {
      inView,
      canPlay,
      userPaused: false,
      autoplayBlocked: false,
      reducedMotion,
    },
  };

  const listeners = new Set<HeroVideoListener>();

  return {
    getSnapshot(): HeroVideoSnapshot {
      return snapshot;
    },
    matches(target: HeroVideoState): boolean {
      return snapshot.state === target;
    },
    send(event: HeroVideoEvent): HeroVideoSnapshot {
      const prev = snapshot;
      const next = transitionHeroVideo(prev, event);
      snapshot = next;
      if (
        next.state !== prev.state ||
        next.context.inView !== prev.context.inView ||
        next.context.canPlay !== prev.context.canPlay ||
        next.context.userPaused !== prev.context.userPaused ||
        next.context.autoplayBlocked !== prev.context.autoplayBlocked
      ) {
        listeners.forEach((fn) => fn(next));
      }
      return next;
    },
    subscribe(listener: HeroVideoListener): () => void {
      listeners.add(listener);
      return () => {
        listeners.delete(listener);
      };
    },
  };
}

--- src\motion\setup.ts ---

import { gsap } from 'gsap';
import { ScrollTrigger } from 'gsap/ScrollTrigger';
import {
  ease,
  isTouch,
  onPage,
  onScroll,
  prefersReducedMotion,
  scrollToTarget,
  setLoaderExit,
  setTransition,
  startScroll,
  stopScroll,
  studioAudio,
} from '../engine';
import { site } from '../data/site';
import { createHeroVideoMachine, type HeroVideoSnapshot } from './heroVideoMachine';
import { initReactiveText } from './textMotion';
import { initWorksPage, initWorksDetailPage } from './works';

gsap.registerPlugin(ScrollTrigger);

function initButtonFills() {
  if (isTouch() || prefersReducedMotion()) return;
  const off: (() => void)[] = [];
  document.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[data-fill]').forEach((btn) => {
    const fill = btn.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.btn__fill');
    if (!fill) return;
    const at = (e: PointerEvent) => {
      const r = btn.getBoundingClientRect();
      return `${e.clientX - r.left}px ${e.clientY - r.top}px`;
    };
    let closed = true;
    const open = (pos: string) => {
      if (closed) gsap.set(fill, { clipPath: `circle(0% at ${pos})` });
      closed = false;
      btn.classList.add('is-filled');
      gsap.to(fill, { clipPath: `circle(155% at ${pos})`, duration: 0.5, ease: ease.soft, overwrite: true });
    };
    const close = (pos: string) => {
      btn.classList.remove('is-filled');
      gsap.to(fill, {
        clipPath: `circle(0% at ${pos})`,
        duration: 0.4,
        ease: ease.soft,
        overwrite: true,
        onComplete: () => { closed = true; },
      });
    };
    const pe = (e: PointerEvent) => { open(at(e)); studioAudio.tick(610, 0.02); };
    const pl = (e: PointerEvent) => close(at(e));
    const fc = () => btn.matches(':focus-visible') && open('50% 50%');
    const bl = () => close('50% 50%');
    btn.addEventListener('pointerenter', pe);
    btn.addEventListener('pointerleave', pl);
    btn.addEventListener('focus', fc);
    btn.addEventListener('blur', bl);
    off.push(() => {
      btn.removeEventListener('pointerenter', pe);
      btn.removeEventListener('pointerleave', pl);
      btn.removeEventListener('focus', fc);
      btn.removeEventListener('blur', bl);
    });
  });
  return () => off.forEach((f) => f());
}

function initHeaderControls() {
  let darkZones: Array<{ top: number; bottom: number }> = [];
  let heroThreshold = window.innerHeight * 0.72;
  let prevScrolled: boolean | null = null;
  let prevScrolledHero: boolean | null = null;
  let prevOverDark: boolean | null = null;

  const header = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.site-header');
  const heroHeader = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.site-header--hero');
  const syncScrolled = (y: number) => {
    const scrolled = y > 24;
    if (scrolled !== prevScrolled) {
      prevScrolled = scrolled;
      header?.setAttribute('data-scrolled', String(scrolled));
    }
    const scrolledHero = y > heroThreshold;
    if (scrolledHero !== prevScrolledHero) {
      prevScrolledHero = scrolledHero;
      heroHeader?.setAttribute('data-scrolled-hero', String(scrolledHero));
    }
  };

  const measureZones = () => {
    const sy = window.scrollY;
    heroThreshold = window.innerHeight * 0.72;
    darkZones = [...document.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('.showroom-bridge-wrap, .showroom-inline-track')].map((zone) => {
      const r = zone.getBoundingClientRect();
      return { top: r.top + sy, bottom: r.bottom + sy };
    });
    syncScrolled(sy);
  };
  measureZones();
  addEventListener('resize', measureZones, { passive: true });
  ScrollTrigger.addEventListener('refresh', measureZones);

  const syncHeaderZone = (y: number) => {
    let overDark = false;
    for (const z of darkZones) {
      if (z.top <= y + 72 && z.bottom >= y + 24) {
        overDark = true;
        break;
      }
    }
    if (overDark !== prevOverDark) {
      prevOverDark = overDark;
      document.documentElement.classList.toggle('header-dark', overDark);
    }
  };
  syncHeaderZone(window.scrollY);
  document.documentElement.classList.remove('frame-away');

  const offScroll = onScroll(({ y }) => {
    syncHeaderZone(y);
    syncScrolled(y);
  });

  const soundBtn = document.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-sound-toggle]');
  const updateSound = () => {
    if (!soundBtn) return;
    const on = studioAudio.isEnabled();
    soundBtn.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(on));
    const lbl = soundBtn.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-sound-label]');
    if (lbl) lbl.textContent = on ? 'Sound on' : 'Sound';
  };
  const onSoundClick = () => {
    studioAudio.toggle();
    updateSound();
  };
  soundBtn?.addEventListener('click', onSoundClick);
  const offSoundSync = studioAudio.onChange(() => updateSound());
  updateSound();

  const menuBtn = document.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-menu-open]');
  const menuClose = document.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-menu-close]');
  const menu = document.getElementById('mobile-menu');
  const openMenu = () => {
    if (!menu || !menuBtn) return;
    menu.hidden = false;
    menuBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'true');
    stopScroll();
    studioAudio.tick(480, 0.025);
    if (!prefersReducedMotion()) {
      gsap.fromTo(menu, { clipPath: 'inset(0% 0% 100% 0%)' }, { clipPath: 'inset(0% 0% 0% 0%)', duration: 0.55, ease: ease.inOut });
    }
    menu.querySelector<HTMLElement>('a, button')?.focus();
  };
  const closeMenu = () => {
    if (!menu || !menuBtn || menu.hidden) return;
    menuBtn.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
    const done = () => {
      menu.hidden = true;
      const consultOpen = drawer && !drawer.hidden;
      if (!consultOpen) {
        startScroll();
        menuBtn.focus();
      }
    };
    if (!prefersReducedMotion()) {
      gsap.to(menu, { clipPath: 'inset(0% 0% 100% 0%)', duration: 0.45, ease: ease.inOut, onComplete: done });
    } else done();
  };
  menuBtn?.addEventListener('click', openMenu);
  menuClose?.addEventListener('click', closeMenu);
  menu?.querySelectorAll('a, button[data-open-drawer]').forEach((el) => el.addEventListener('click', closeMenu));

  const drawer = document.getElementById('consult-drawer');
  const drawerPanel = drawer?.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.drawer__panel');
  const openBtns = document.querySelectorAll<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-open-drawer]');
  const closeBtn = drawer?.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-close-drawer]');
  const backdrop = drawer?.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-drawer-backdrop]');
  let lastTrigger: HTMLElement | null = null;
  let activeContext = '';

  const form = drawer?.querySelector<HTMLFormElement>('[data-wa-form]');
  const preview = drawer?.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-wa-preview]');
  const sendLink = drawer?.querySelector<HTMLAnchorElement>('[data-wa-send]');
  const syncWaMessage = () => {
    if (!form) return;
    const data = new FormData(form);
    const space = String(data.get('space') || 'Turnkey Home Interior');
    const city = String(data.get('city') || 'Biratnagar');
    const area = String(data.get('area') || '2BHK / 3BHK Residence');
    const timeline = String(data.get('timeline') || 'Within 2 months');
    const introLine = activeContext
      ? `Namaste Riya ji, I saw "${activeContext}" on the Rubiconius website`
      : 'Namaste Riya ji, I saw the Rubiconius website';
    const msg = `${introLine} and would like to discuss a ${space} project in ${city} (scope: ${area}, timeline: ${timeline}).`;
    if (preview) preview.textContent = msg;
    if (sendLink) sendLink.href = `https://wa.me/${site.whatsappNumber}?text=${encodeURIComponent(msg)}`;
  };
  form?.addEventListener('input', syncWaMessage);
  form?.addEventListener('change', syncWaMessage);
  syncWaMessage();

  const openDrawer = (trigger?: HTMLElement) => {
    if (!drawer) return;
    lastTrigger = trigger || (document.activeElement as HTMLElement);
    activeContext = trigger?.dataset.drawerContext?.trim() || '';
    syncWaMessage();
    drawer.hidden = false;
    stopScroll();
    studioAudio.tick(680, 0.03);
    if (drawerPanel && !prefersReducedMotion()) {
      gsap.fromTo(drawerPanel, { yPercent: 100 }, { yPercent: 0, duration: 0.55, ease: ease.out });
    }
    drawer.querySelector<HTMLElement>('button, input, select')?.focus();
  };
  const closeDrawer = () => {
    if (!drawer || drawer.hidden) return;
    const finishClose = () => {
      drawer.hidden = true;
      startScroll();
      lastTrigger?.focus();
    };
    if (drawerPanel && !prefersReducedMotion()) {
      gsap.to(drawerPanel, { yPercent: 100, duration: 0.4, ease: ease.soft, onComplete: finishClose });
    } else finishClose();
  };
  const openHandlers: Array<() => void> = [];
  openBtns.forEach((b) => {
    const h = () => openDrawer(b);
    b.addEventListener('click', h);
    openHandlers.push(() => b.removeEventListener('click', h));
  });
  closeBtn?.addEventListener('click', closeDrawer);
  backdrop?.addEventListener('click', closeDrawer);

  const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
    if (e.key === 'Escape') {
      if (drawer && !drawer.hidden) closeDrawer();
      else if (menu && !menu.hidden) closeMenu();
    }
  };
  document.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);

  const copyBtn = document.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-copy-phone]');
  const copyDefaultLabel = copyBtn?.textContent?.trim() || '';
  let copyTimer: number | undefined;
  const onCopyPhone = async () => {
    if (!copyBtn) return;
    const phone = copyBtn.dataset.copyPhone || site.phoneDisplay;
    let copied = false;
    try {
      if (navigator.clipboard && window.isSecureContext) {
        await navigator.clipboard.writeText(phone);
        copied = true;
      }
    } catch {}
    if (!copied) {
      const ta = document.createElement('textarea');
      ta.value = phone;
      ta.setAttribute('readonly', '');
      ta.style.position = 'fixed';
      ta.style.opacity = '0';
      ta.style.pointerEvents = 'none';
      document.body.appendChild(ta);
      ta.select();
      try {
        copied = document.execCommand('copy');
      } catch {}
      ta.remove();
    }
    studioAudio.tick(640, 0.025);
    copyBtn.classList.add('is-copied');
    copyBtn.textContent = `Copied \u00b7 ${phone}`;
    window.clearTimeout(copyTimer);
    copyTimer = window.setTimeout(() => {
      copyBtn.classList.remove('is-copied');
      copyBtn.textContent = copyDefaultLabel || `Copy Studio Number (${phone})`;
    }, 2200);
  };
  copyBtn?.addEventListener('click', onCopyPhone);

  const backTopBtn = document.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-back-to-top]');
  const onBackToTop = () => {
    studioAudio.tick(460, 0.02);
    scrollToTarget(0);
  };
  backTopBtn?.addEventListener('click', onBackToTop);

  return () => {
    offScroll();
    offSoundSync();
    removeEventListener('resize', measureZones);
    ScrollTrigger.removeEventListener('refresh', measureZones);
    soundBtn?.removeEventListener('click', onSoundClick);
    menuBtn?.removeEventListener('click', openMenu);
    menuClose?.removeEventListener('click', closeMenu);
    openHandlers.forEach((u) => u());
    closeBtn?.removeEventListener('click', closeDrawer);
    backdrop?.removeEventListener('click', closeDrawer);
    form?.removeEventListener('input', syncWaMessage);
    form?.removeEventListener('change', syncWaMessage);
    document.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey);
    window.clearTimeout(copyTimer);
    copyBtn?.removeEventListener('click', onCopyPhone);
    backTopBtn?.removeEventListener('click', onBackToTop);
  };
}

function initHeroVideo() {
  const video = document.querySelector<HTMLVideoElement>('[data-hero-video]');
  if (!video) return;

  const hero = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.cinema-hero');
  const toggleBtn = document.querySelector<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-hero-video-toggle]');
  const reduced = prefersReducedMotion();
  const machine = createHeroVideoMachine({
    reducedMotion: reduced,
    canPlay: !reduced && video.readyState >= 3,
  });

  const abort = new AbortController();
  const { signal } = abort;
  let playSeq = 0;

  const syncButton = (snap: HeroVideoSnapshot) => {
    if (!toggleBtn) return;
    if (snap.state === 'reducedMotion') {
      toggleBtn.hidden = true;
      toggleBtn.setAttribute('aria-pressed', 'true');
      return;
    }
    toggleBtn.hidden = false;
    const isPaused = snap.state === 'pausedByUser' || snap.context.autoplayBlocked;
    toggleBtn.textContent = isPaused ? 'Play film' : 'Pause film';
    toggleBtn.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(isPaused));
    toggleBtn.setAttribute('aria-label', isPaused ? 'Play background film' : 'Pause background film');
  };

  const applySnapshot = (snap: HeroVideoSnapshot) => {
    syncButton(snap);

    switch (snap.state) {
      case 'reducedMotion': {
        playSeq++;
        video.pause();
        video.classList.remove('is-playing');
        break;
      }
      case 'poster': {
        playSeq++;
        video.pause();
        video.classList.remove('is-playing');
        break;
      }
      case 'pausedByUser': {
        playSeq++;
        video.pause();
        if (!snap.context.canPlay || snap.context.autoplayBlocked) {
          video.classList.remove('is-playing');
        }
        break;
      }
      case 'pausedOffscreen': {
        playSeq++;
        video.pause();
        break;
      }
      case 'playing': {
        const currentSeq = ++playSeq;
        if (!video.paused && video.readyState >= 3) {
          video.classList.add('is-playing');
          break;
        }
        try {
          const p = video.play();
          if (p && typeof p.then === 'function') {
            p.then(() => {
              if (!signal.aborted && currentSeq === playSeq && machine.matches('playing')) {
                video.classList.add('is-playing');
              }
            }).catch(() => {
              if (!signal.aborted && currentSeq === playSeq) {
                machine.send('PLAY_FAILED');
              }
            });
          } else {
            video.classList.add('is-playing');
          }
        } catch {
          machine.send('PLAY_FAILED');
        }
        break;
      }
    }
  };

  const unsubscribe = machine.subscribe(applySnapshot);
  applySnapshot(machine.getSnapshot());

  if (!reduced) {
    if (video.readyState >= 3) {
      machine.send('CAN_PLAY');
    }

    video.addEventListener('canplay', () => machine.send('CAN_PLAY'), { signal });
    video.addEventListener('canplaythrough', () => machine.send('CAN_PLAY'), { signal });
    video.addEventListener(
      'playing',
      () => {
        if (machine.matches('poster')) {
          machine.send('CAN_PLAY');
        }
        if (machine.matches('playing')) {
          video.classList.add('is-playing');
        }
      },
      { signal }
    );
    video.addEventListener('error', () => machine.send('PLAY_FAILED'), { signal });
  }

  let io: IntersectionObserver | null = null;
  if (!reduced && 'IntersectionObserver' in window) {
    io = new IntersectionObserver(
      (entries) => {
        const entry = entries[0];
        if (!entry) return;
        machine.send(entry.isIntersecting ? 'IN_VIEW' : 'OUT_OF_VIEW');
      },
      { threshold: 0.35 }
    );
    io.observe(hero || video);
  }

  toggleBtn?.addEventListener(
    'click',
    () => {
      studioAudio.tick(540, 0.02);
      machine.send('USER_TOGGLE');
    },
    { signal }
  );

  return () => {
    unsubscribe();
    io?.disconnect();
    abort.abort();
    video.pause();
  };
}

function initEditorialTextMotion() {
  const win = window as unknown as Record<string, unknown>;
  win.gsap = gsap;
  win.ScrollTrigger = ScrollTrigger;
  if (prefersReducedMotion()) return;

  const cleanups: Array<() => void> = [];
  const showroomRoots = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('.showroom-root'));
  if (showroomRoots.length && 'IntersectionObserver' in window) {
    const io = new IntersectionObserver(
      (entries) => {
        entries.forEach((entry) => {
          entry.target.classList.toggle('is-inview', entry.isIntersecting);
        });
      },
      { rootMargin: '150px 0px' }
    );
    showroomRoots.forEach((el) => io.observe(el));
    cleanups.push(() => io.disconnect());
  }

  return () => {
    cleanups.forEach((fn) => fn());
  };
}

function initStudioLocator() {
  const root = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-studio-locator]');
  if (!root) return;

  const tabs = Array.from(root.querySelectorAll<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-locator-tab]'));
  const iframe = root.querySelector<HTMLIFrameElement>('[data-locator-iframe]');
  const directions = root.querySelector<HTMLAnchorElement>('[data-locator-directions]');
  const addrEl = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-locator-active-addr]');
  const coordsEl = root.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-locator-active-coords]');
  if (!tabs.length || !iframe) return;

  let loadTimer: number | undefined;
  const finishLoad = () => {
    window.clearTimeout(loadTimer);
    iframe.classList.remove('is-loading');
  };
  iframe.addEventListener('load', finishLoad);

  const handlers = tabs.map((tab) => {
    const onClick = () => {
      if (tab.classList.contains('is-active')) return;
      tabs.forEach((t) => {
        const active = t === tab;
        t.classList.toggle('is-active', active);
        t.setAttribute('aria-selected', String(active));
      });
      studioAudio.tick(520, 0.02);

      const embedUrl = tab.dataset.embedUrl;
      const mapUrl = tab.dataset.mapUrl;
      const addr = tab.dataset.addr;
      const coords = tab.dataset.coords;
      const city = tab.dataset.city;

      if (addr && addrEl) addrEl.textContent = addr;
      if (coords && coordsEl) coordsEl.textContent = coords;
      if (mapUrl && directions) directions.href = mapUrl;
      if (city) iframe.title = `Rubiconius Studio Map — ${city}`;
      if (embedUrl && iframe.src !== embedUrl) {
        iframe.classList.add('is-loading');
        window.clearTimeout(loadTimer);
        loadTimer = window.setTimeout(finishLoad, 900);
        iframe.src = embedUrl;
      }
    };
    tab.addEventListener('click', onClick);
    return () => tab.removeEventListener('click', onClick);
  });

  return () => {
    window.clearTimeout(loadTimer);
    iframe.removeEventListener('load', finishLoad);
    handlers.forEach((fn) => fn());
  };
}

export function setupMotion() {
  setLoaderExit((root) => {
    const symWrap = root.querySelector('.loader__symbol-wrap');
    const symbol = root.querySelector('.loader__symbol');
    const arch = root.querySelector('.sym__arch');
    const core = root.querySelector('.sym__core');
    const ring = root.querySelector('.sym__ring');
    const ticks = root.querySelector('.sym__ticks');
    const word = root.querySelector('[data-loader-word]');
    const bar = root.querySelector('.loader__bar');

    const tl = gsap.timeline();

    return tl
      .set([arch, core], { fillOpacity: 1, strokeDashoffset: 0, transformOrigin: 'center center' })
      .set([ring, ticks], { svgOrigin: '60 70' })
      .to(bar, { scaleX: 1, duration: 0.1, ease: ease.soft })
      // Beat 1: Architectural split & compass lock
      .to(arch, { y: -6, scale: 1.04, duration: 0.18, ease: ease.out }, '<')
      .to(core, { y: 4, scale: 1.08, duration: 0.18, ease: ease.out }, '<')
      .to(ring, { scale: 0.92, opacity: 1, duration: 0.18, ease: ease.out }, '<')
      .to(ticks, { rotation: 90, opacity: 1, duration: 0.2, ease: ease.inOut }, '<')
      // Beat 2: Precision lockup + wordmark & ring release
      .to([arch, core], { y: 0, scale: 1.1, duration: 0.14, ease: ease.inOut })
      .to([ring, ticks], { scale: 1.25, autoAlpha: 0, duration: 0.18, ease: ease.out }, '<')
      .to(word, { y: -8, letterSpacing: '0.36em', autoAlpha: 0, duration: 0.16, ease: ease.inOut }, '<')
      .to(bar, { autoAlpha: 0, duration: 0.1 }, '<')
      // Beat 3: Subtle compositor fade-out (avoids 14x SVG raster spike)
      .to(symbol || symWrap, { scale: 1.15, autoAlpha: 0, duration: 0.22, ease: ease.out }, '-=0.04')
      .to(root, { autoAlpha: 0, duration: 0.2, ease: ease.out }, '<');
  });

  setTransition({
    leave: (c) => {
      const blades = c.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('.curtain__blade');
      const crest = c.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.curtain__crest');
      const line = c.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.curtain__line');
      gsap.killTweensOf([c, blades, crest, line]);
      return gsap.timeline()
        .set(c, { autoAlpha: 1 })
        .set(blades, { transformOrigin: 'bottom center', scaleY: 0 })
        .fromTo(
          blades,
          { scaleY: 0 },
          { scaleY: 1, duration: 0.48, stagger: 0.045, ease: ease.inOut }
        )
        .fromTo(
          crest,
          { autoAlpha: 0, y: 12 },
          { autoAlpha: 1, y: 0, duration: 0.34, ease: ease.out },
          '-=0.22'
        )
        .fromTo(
          line,
          { scaleX: 0 },
          { scaleX: 1, duration: 0.32, ease: ease.out },
          '<+0.06'
        );
    },
    enter: (c) => {
      const blades = c.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('.curtain__blade');
      const crest = c.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.curtain__crest');
      const line = c.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.curtain__line');
      gsap.killTweensOf([c, blades, crest, line]);
      return gsap.timeline()
        .to(line, { scaleX: 0, duration: 0.22, ease: ease.inOut })
        .to(crest, { autoAlpha: 0, y: -12, duration: 0.28, ease: ease.inOut }, '<')
        .set(blades, { transformOrigin: 'top center' })
        .to(
          blades,
          { scaleY: 0, duration: 0.5, stagger: 0.045, ease: ease.inOut },
          '-=0.14'
        )
        .set(blades, { transformOrigin: 'bottom center' })
        .set(c, { autoAlpha: 0 });
    },
  });

  onPage(() => {
    const showroomRoot = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-showroom-root]');
    if (showroomRoot && showroomRoot.dataset.showroomMode !== 'inline') {
      document.documentElement.classList.remove('header-dark', 'frame-away');
      showroomRoot.classList.add('is-inview');
      let disposed = false;
      let handle: { dispose: () => void } | null = null;
      import('../engine/showroom').then(({ mountShowroom }) => {
        if (disposed) return;
        handle = mountShowroom(showroomRoot, { mode: 'fullscreen' });
      });
      return () => {
        disposed = true;
        showroomRoot.classList.remove('is-inview');
        handle?.dispose();
      };
    }

    const cleanFill = initButtonFills();
    const cleanHeader = initHeaderControls();
    const cleanHeroVideo = initHeroVideo();
    const cleanReactiveText = initReactiveText();
    const cleanEditorial = initEditorialTextMotion();
    const cleanWorks = initWorksPage();
    const cleanWorksDetail = initWorksDetailPage();
    const cleanLocator = initStudioLocator();

    return () => {
      cleanLocator?.();
      cleanWorks?.();
      cleanWorksDetail?.();
      cleanFill?.();
      cleanHeader?.();
      cleanHeroVideo?.();
      cleanReactiveText?.();
      cleanEditorial?.();
    };
  });
}

--- src\motion\textMotion.ts ---

import { gsap } from 'gsap';
import { prefersReducedMotion, scrollState } from '../engine';

const getWordsFromNode = (node: Node, out: Array<{ type: 'word'; text: string } | { type: 'space'; text: string } | { type: 'br' }>) => {
  if (node.nodeType === Node.ELEMENT_NODE) {
    const elem = node as Element;
    if (elem.tagName === 'BR') {
      out.push({ type: 'br' });
      return;
    }
    Array.from(elem.childNodes).forEach((child) => getWordsFromNode(child, out));
    return;
  }

  if (node.nodeType === Node.TEXT_NODE) {
    const raw = node.textContent || '';
    const tokens = raw.split(/(\s+)/);
    for (const token of tokens) {
      if (!token) continue;
      if (/^\s+$/.test(token)) {
        out.push({ type: 'space', text: ' ' });
      } else {
        out.push({ type: 'word', text: token });
      }
    }
  }
};

function setupProximityElement(
  el: HTMLElement,
  signal: AbortSignal,
  restoreTasks: Array<() => void>
) {
  const rawText = (el.textContent || '').trim();
  if (!rawText) return;

  const originalHTML = el.innerHTML;
  const hadAriaLabel = el.hasAttribute('aria-label');
  const prevAriaLabel = el.getAttribute('aria-label');

  if (!hadAriaLabel) {
    el.setAttribute('aria-label', rawText);
  }
  el.dataset.rxBound = '1';

  const fragment = document.createDocumentFragment();
  const charElements: HTMLSpanElement[] = [];

  for (const ch of Array.from(rawText)) {
    const span = document.createElement('span');
    span.className = 'rx-char';
    span.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
    span.textContent = ch === ' ' ? '\u00A0' : ch;
    fragment.appendChild(span);
    charElements.push(span);
  }

  el.replaceChildren(fragment);

  let box: { left: number; top: number; width: number; height: number } | null = null;
  const litState: boolean[] = new Array(charElements.length).fill(false);
  let isRippling = false;
  let rippleCooldownId: number | undefined;
  let pendingPoint: { x: number; y: number } | null = null;
  let moveRafId = 0;

  const measureBox = () => {
    if (signal.aborted || !el.isConnected) return;
    const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
    box = {
      left: r.left,
      top: r.top + scrollState().y,
      width: r.width,
      height: r.height,
    };
  };

  const invalidateMeasure = () => {
    box = null;
  };
  window.addEventListener('resize', invalidateMeasure, { passive: true, signal });

  const setLit = (idx: number, lit: boolean) => {
    if (litState[idx] === lit) return;
    litState[idx] = lit;
    charElements[idx].classList.toggle('is-lit', lit);
  };

  const resetChars = () => {
    cancelAnimationFrame(moveRafId);
    moveRafId = 0;
    pendingPoint = null;
    if (isRippling) return;
    for (let i = 0; i < charElements.length; i++) {
      if (charElements[i].style.transform) charElements[i].style.transform = '';
      setLit(i, false);
    }
  };

  const applyProximityNow = (clientX: number, clientY: number) => {
    if (isRippling || signal.aborted) return;
    if (!box) measureBox();
    if (!box || box.width <= 0) return;

    const count = charElements.length;
    const avgWidth = Math.max(20, box.width / count);
    const radius = Math.max(96, Math.min(250, avgWidth * 2.65));
    const maxLift = Math.max(12, Math.min(24, box.height * 0.16));
    const pointerDocY = clientY + scrollState().y;
    const centerY = box.top + box.height / 2;

    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      const charCenterX = box.left + ((i + 0.5) / count) * box.width;
      const dx = charCenterX - clientX;
      const dy = centerY - pointerDocY;
      const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy * 0.72);
      if (dist < radius) {
        const t = 1 - dist / radius;
        const falloff = t * t * (3 - 2 * t);
        charElements[i].style.transform =
          falloff > 0.02 ? `translate3d(0, ${(-maxLift * falloff).toFixed(1)}px, 0)` : '';
        setLit(i, falloff > 0.24);
      } else {
        if (charElements[i].style.transform) charElements[i].style.transform = '';
        setLit(i, false);
      }
    }
  };

  const scheduleProximity = (clientX: number, clientY: number) => {
    pendingPoint = { x: clientX, y: clientY };
    if (moveRafId) return;
    moveRafId = requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      moveRafId = 0;
      const pt = pendingPoint;
      pendingPoint = null;
      if (pt) applyProximityNow(pt.x, pt.y);
    });
  };

  const findNearestCharIndex = (clientX: number): number => {
    if (!box) measureBox();
    if (!box || box.width <= 0) return 0;
    return Math.max(0, Math.min(charElements.length - 1, Math.floor(((clientX - box.left) / box.width) * charElements.length)));
  };

  el.addEventListener(
    'pointerenter',
    () => {
      measureBox();
    },
    { passive: true, signal }
  );

  el.addEventListener(
    'pointermove',
    (e: PointerEvent) => {
      if (e.pointerType === 'touch') return;
      scheduleProximity(e.clientX, e.clientY);
    },
    { passive: true, signal }
  );

  el.addEventListener(
    'pointerleave',
    () => {
      isRippling = false;
      window.clearTimeout(rippleCooldownId);
      resetChars();
    },
    { passive: true, signal }
  );

  el.addEventListener(
    'touchstart',
    (e: TouchEvent) => {
      measureBox();
      const touch = e.touches[0];
      if (touch) scheduleProximity(touch.clientX, touch.clientY);
    },
    { passive: true, signal }
  );

  el.addEventListener(
    'touchmove',
    (e: TouchEvent) => {
      const touch = e.touches[0];
      if (touch) scheduleProximity(touch.clientX, touch.clientY);
    },
    { passive: true, signal }
  );

  el.addEventListener(
    'touchend',
    () => {
      resetChars();
    },
    { passive: true, signal }
  );

  el.addEventListener(
    'touchcancel',
    () => {
      isRippling = false;
      window.clearTimeout(rippleCooldownId);
      resetChars();
    },
    { passive: true, signal }
  );

  el.addEventListener(
    'pointerdown',
    (e: PointerEvent) => {
      if (e.button > 0) return;
      const tappedIndex = findNearestCharIndex(e.clientX);
      isRippling = true;
      window.clearTimeout(rippleCooldownId);

      for (let i = 0; i < charElements.length; i++) {
        const dist = Math.abs(i - tappedIndex);
        setLit(i, dist <= 1);
        const lift = dist === 0 ? -18 : dist === 1 ? -9 : -3;
        charElements[i].style.transform = `translate3d(0, ${lift}px, 0)`;
      }

      rippleCooldownId = window.setTimeout(() => {
        isRippling = false;
        resetChars();
      }, 260);
    },
    { passive: true, signal }
  );

  restoreTasks.push(() => {
    cancelAnimationFrame(moveRafId);
    window.clearTimeout(rippleCooldownId);
    delete el.dataset.rxBound;
    if (!hadAriaLabel) {
      el.removeAttribute('aria-label');
    } else if (prevAriaLabel !== null) {
      el.setAttribute('aria-label', prevAriaLabel);
    }
    el.innerHTML = originalHTML;
  });
}

function setupWordsElement(
  el: HTMLElement,
  signal: AbortSignal,
  restoreTasks: Array<() => void>
) {
  const rawTokens: Array<{ type: 'word'; text: string } | { type: 'space'; text: string } | { type: 'br' }> = [];
  Array.from(el.childNodes).forEach((child) => getWordsFromNode(child, rawTokens));
  if (!rawTokens.some((t) => t.type === 'word')) return;

  const originalHTML = el.innerHTML;
  el.dataset.rxBound = '1';

  // Trim leading/trailing pure whitespace tokens while preserving internal spaces & <br>
  let start = 0;
  let end = rawTokens.length - 1;
  while (start <= end && rawTokens[start].type === 'space') start++;
  while (end >= start && rawTokens[end].type === 'space') end--;

  const fragment = document.createDocumentFragment();
  let wordCount = 0;

  for (let i = start; i <= end; i++) {
    const tok = rawTokens[i];
    if (tok.type === 'br') {
      fragment.appendChild(document.createElement('br'));
    } else if (tok.type === 'space') {
      const prev = rawTokens[i - 1];
      const next = rawTokens[i + 1];
      if (prev?.type === 'br' || next?.type === 'br') continue;
      fragment.appendChild(document.createTextNode(' '));
    } else {
      const span = document.createElement('span');
      span.className = 'rx-word';
      span.textContent = tok.text;
      fragment.appendChild(span);
      wordCount++;
    }
  }

  el.replaceChildren(fragment);
  if (!wordCount) return;

  let activeWord: HTMLElement | null = null;
  let touchResetTimer: number | undefined;

  const setActiveWord = (word: HTMLElement | null) => {
    if (activeWord && activeWord !== word) {
      activeWord.style.transform = '';
      activeWord.classList.remove('is-active');
    }
    activeWord = word;
    if (word) {
      word.style.transform = 'translate3d(0, -3px, 0)';
      word.classList.add('is-active');
    }
  };

  el.addEventListener(
    'pointerover',
    (e: PointerEvent) => {
      if (e.pointerType === 'touch') return;
      const word = (e.target as Element | null)?.closest<HTMLElement>('.rx-word') ?? null;
      if (word && word !== activeWord) setActiveWord(word);
    },
    { passive: true, signal }
  );

  el.addEventListener(
    'pointerout',
    (e: PointerEvent) => {
      if (e.pointerType === 'touch') return;
      const word = (e.target as Element | null)?.closest<HTMLElement>('.rx-word');
      if (!word || word !== activeWord) return;
      const related = e.relatedTarget as Node | null;
      if (related && word.contains(related)) return;
      setActiveWord(null);
    },
    { passive: true, signal }
  );

  el.addEventListener(
    'pointerleave',
    (e: PointerEvent) => {
      if (e.pointerType === 'touch') return;
      setActiveWord(null);
    },
    { passive: true, signal }
  );

  el.addEventListener(
    'touchstart',
    (e: TouchEvent) => {
      window.clearTimeout(touchResetTimer);
      const word = (e.target as Element | null)?.closest<HTMLElement>('.rx-word') ?? null;
      setActiveWord(word);
    },
    { passive: true, signal }
  );

  el.addEventListener(
    'touchend',
    () => {
      window.clearTimeout(touchResetTimer);
      touchResetTimer = window.setTimeout(() => setActiveWord(null), 220);
    },
    { passive: true, signal }
  );

  el.addEventListener(
    'touchcancel',
    () => {
      window.clearTimeout(touchResetTimer);
      setActiveWord(null);
    },
    { passive: true, signal }
  );

  restoreTasks.push(() => {
    window.clearTimeout(touchResetTimer);
    delete el.dataset.rxBound;
    el.innerHTML = originalHTML;
  });
}

function setupRollElement(
  el: HTMLElement,
  signal: AbortSignal,
  restoreTasks: Array<() => void>
) {
  const text = (el.textContent || '').trim();
  if (!text) return;

  const originalHTML = el.innerHTML;
  el.dataset.rxBound = '1';

  let chars: HTMLSpanElement[] | null = null;
  const ensureBuilt = () => {
    if (chars) return chars;
    const sr = document.createElement('span');
    sr.className = 'sr-only';
    sr.textContent = text;

    const track = document.createElement('span');
    track.className = 'rx-roll';
    track.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');

    chars = [];
    for (const ch of Array.from(text)) {
      const glyph = ch === ' ' ? '\u00A0' : ch;
      const outer = document.createElement('span');
      outer.className = 'rx-roll__char';
      outer.textContent = glyph;

      const dup = document.createElement('span');
      dup.className = 'rx-roll__dup';
      dup.textContent = glyph;
      outer.appendChild(dup);

      track.appendChild(outer);
      chars.push(outer);
    }

    el.replaceChildren(sr, track);
    return chars;
  };

  const trigger = el.closest<HTMLElement>('a, button') ?? el;
  const playRoll = () => {
    const builtChars = ensureBuilt();
    gsap.fromTo(
      builtChars,
      { yPercent: 0 },
      {
        yPercent: -100,
        stagger: 0.015,
        duration: 0.34,
        ease: 'power3.out',
        overwrite: true,
        onComplete: () => {
          gsap.set(builtChars, { yPercent: 0 });
        },
      }
    );
  };

  trigger.addEventListener(
    'pointerenter',
    (e: PointerEvent) => {
      if (e.pointerType === 'touch') return;
      playRoll();
    },
    { passive: true, signal }
  );

  trigger.addEventListener(
    'touchstart',
    () => {
      playRoll();
    },
    { passive: true, signal }
  );

  restoreTasks.push(() => {
    delete el.dataset.rxBound;
    el.innerHTML = originalHTML;
  });
}

export function initReactiveText(): (() => void) | void {
  if (prefersReducedMotion()) return;

  const targets = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[data-text-reactive]'));
  if (!targets.length) return;

  const controller = new AbortController();
  const { signal } = controller;
  const restoreTasks: Array<() => void> = [];

  const ctx = gsap.context(() => {
    for (const el of targets) {
      if (el.dataset.rxBound === '1') continue;
      const mode = el.dataset.textReactive;
      if (mode === 'proximity') {
        setupProximityElement(el, signal, restoreTasks);
      } else if (mode === 'words') {
        setupWordsElement(el, signal, restoreTasks);
      } else if (mode === 'roll') {
        setupRollElement(el, signal, restoreTasks);
      }
    }
  }, document);

  let cleaned = false;
  const teardown = () => {
    if (cleaned) return;
    cleaned = true;
    controller.abort();
    ctx.revert();
    restoreTasks.splice(0).forEach((fn) => fn());
  };

  document.addEventListener('astro:before-swap', teardown, { once: true, signal });

  return teardown;
}

--- src\motion\works.ts ---

import { gsap } from 'gsap';
import { ScrollTrigger } from 'gsap/ScrollTrigger';
import { prefersReducedMotion, resizeScroll } from '../engine';

gsap.registerPlugin(ScrollTrigger);

/** Exact mathematical cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1) weighted architectural curve */
function makeCubicBezier(p1x: number, p1y: number, p2x: number, p2y: number) {
  const cx = 3 * p1x;
  const bx = 3 * (p2x - p1x) - cx;
  const ax = 1 - cx - bx;
  const cy = 3 * p1y;
  const by = 3 * (p2y - p1y) - cy;
  const ay = 1 - cy - by;

  const sampleX = (t: number) => ((ax * t + bx) * t + cx) * t;
  const sampleY = (t: number) => ((ay * t + by) * t + cy) * t;
  const sampleDerivX = (t: number) => (3 * ax * t + 2 * bx) * t + cx;

  return (x: number): number => {
    if (x <= 0) return 0;
    if (x >= 1) return 1;
    let t = x;
    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) {
      const err = sampleX(t) - x;
      if (Math.abs(err) < 1e-5) break;
      const d = sampleDerivX(t);
      if (Math.abs(d) < 1e-6) break;
      t -= err / d;
    }
    return sampleY(Math.max(0, Math.min(1, t)));
  };
}

const MONOGRAPH_EASE = makeCubicBezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1);

export function initWorksPage(): (() => void) | void {
  const worksRoot = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-works-root]');
  if (!worksRoot) return;

  const controller = new AbortController();
  const { signal } = controller;
  const reduced = prefersReducedMotion();
  const finePointer = matchMedia('(hover: hover) and (pointer: fine)').matches;

  const editorialPanel = worksRoot.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-works-panel="editorial"]');
  const indexPanel = worksRoot.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-works-panel="index"]');
  const viewButtons = Array.from(
    worksRoot.querySelectorAll<HTMLButtonElement>('[data-works-view-btn]')
  );

  const indexList = worksRoot.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-project-index]');
  const follower = worksRoot.querySelector<HTMLElement>('[data-cursor-follow]');
  const rows = Array.from(worksRoot.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[data-index-row]'));
  const slides = Array.from(worksRoot.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[data-follow-slide]'));

  let activeView: 'editorial' | 'index' = 'editorial';
  let toggleTween: gsap.core.Tween | null = null;

  const syncScrollEngines = () => {
    requestAnimationFrame(() => {
      resizeScroll();
      ScrollTrigger.refresh();
    });
  };

  const resetFollower = (immediate = false) => {
    rows.forEach((r) => r.classList.remove('is-dimmed', 'is-hovered'));
    if (!follower) return;
    if (immediate || reduced) {
      gsap.set(follower, { opacity: 0, clipPath: 'inset(8% 0% 8% 0%)' });
    } else {
      gsap.to(follower, {
        opacity: 0,
        clipPath: 'inset(8% 0% 8% 0%)',
        duration: 0.3,
        ease: MONOGRAPH_EASE,
        overwrite: 'auto',
      });
    }
  };

  const ctx = gsap.context(() => {
    // 1. Subtle desktop-only parallax within overflow-hidden editorial frames
    if (!reduced && finePointer && window.innerWidth > 768) {
      const parallaxTargets = worksRoot.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('[data-editorial-parallax]');
      parallaxTargets.forEach((el) => {
        const container = el.closest<HTMLElement>('[data-works-spread]') ?? el.parentElement;
        if (!container) return;
        gsap.fromTo(
          el,
          { scale: 1.09, yPercent: -4 },
          {
            scale: 1.09,
            yPercent: 4,
            ease: 'none',
            scrollTrigger: {
              trigger: container,
              start: 'top bottom',
              end: 'bottom top',
              scrub: 0.6,
            },
          }
        );
      });
    }

    // 2. Fine-pointer floating preview & cursor tracking (strictly gated off on touch devices)
    if (!reduced && finePointer && indexList && follower && rows.length > 0) {
      gsap.set(follower, {
        xPercent: -50,
        yPercent: -50,
        opacity: 0,
        clipPath: 'inset(8% 0% 8% 0%)',
        pointerEvents: 'none',
      });

      const xTo = gsap.quickTo(follower, 'x', { duration: 0.35, ease: MONOGRAPH_EASE });
      const yTo = gsap.quickTo(follower, 'y', { duration: 0.35, ease: MONOGRAPH_EASE });

      indexList.addEventListener(
        'pointermove',
        (e: PointerEvent) => {
          if (activeView !== 'index') return;
          xTo(e.clientX);
          yTo(e.clientY);
        },
        { passive: true, signal }
      );

      rows.forEach((row) => {
        const idx = row.dataset.projectIdx;
        row.addEventListener(
          'pointerenter',
          (e: PointerEvent) => {
            if (activeView !== 'index') return;
            xTo(e.clientX);
            yTo(e.clientY);

            rows.forEach((other) => {
              other.classList.toggle('is-hovered', other === row);
              other.classList.toggle('is-dimmed', other !== row);
            });

            slides.forEach((slide) => {
              const active = slide.dataset.followSlide === idx;
              slide.classList.toggle('is-active', active);
            });

            gsap.to(follower, {
              opacity: 1,
              clipPath: 'inset(0% 0% 0% 0%)',
              duration: 0.35,
              ease: MONOGRAPH_EASE,
              overwrite: 'auto',
            });
          },
          { signal }
        );
      });

      indexList.addEventListener(
        'pointerleave',
        () => {
          resetFollower(false);
        },
        { signal }
      );
    }
  }, worksRoot);

  // 3. View Switcher: Editorial | Index (Zero DOM duplication + Lenis/ScrollTrigger resync)
  const setView = (nextView: 'editorial' | 'index') => {
    if (nextView === activeView || !editorialPanel || !indexPanel) return;

    const fromPanel = activeView === 'editorial' ? editorialPanel : indexPanel;
    const toPanel = nextView === 'editorial' ? editorialPanel : indexPanel;
    activeView = nextView;

    viewButtons.forEach((btn) => {
      const isActive = btn.dataset.worksViewBtn === nextView;
      btn.classList.toggle('is-active', isActive);
      btn.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(isActive));
    });

    if (nextView !== 'index') {
      resetFollower(true);
    }

    toggleTween?.kill();

    if (reduced) {
      fromPanel.hidden = true;
      fromPanel.style.opacity = '';
      toPanel.hidden = false;
      toPanel.style.opacity = '1';
      syncScrollEngines();
      return;
    }

    toggleTween = gsap.to(fromPanel, {
      opacity: 0,
      duration: 0.16,
      ease: MONOGRAPH_EASE,
      onComplete: () => {
        fromPanel.hidden = true;
        fromPanel.style.opacity = '';
        toPanel.hidden = false;
        gsap.set(toPanel, { opacity: 0 });
        syncScrollEngines();

        toggleTween = gsap.to(toPanel, {
          opacity: 1,
          duration: 0.35,
          ease: MONOGRAPH_EASE,
          onComplete: () => {
            toPanel.style.opacity = '';
            syncScrollEngines();
          },
        });
      },
    });
  };

  viewButtons.forEach((btn) => {
    btn.addEventListener(
      'click',
      () => {
        const target = btn.dataset.worksViewBtn;
        if (target === 'editorial' || target === 'index') {
          setView(target);
        }
      },
      { signal }
    );
  });

  // 4. Lifecycle-safe teardown across Astro ClientRouter swaps
  let cleaned = false;
  const teardown = () => {
    if (cleaned) return;
    cleaned = true;
    toggleTween?.kill();
    controller.abort();
    ctx.revert();
  };

  document.addEventListener('astro:before-swap', teardown, { once: true, signal });

  return teardown;
}

export function initWorksDetailPage(): (() => void) | void {
  const caseGrid = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.case-grid');
  if (!caseGrid) return;

  const controller = new AbortController();
  const { signal } = controller;
  const reduced = prefersReducedMotion();

  const ctx = gsap.context(() => {
    if (reduced) return;

    const factRows = document.querySelectorAll<HTMLElement>('.case-facts div');
    if (factRows.length > 0) {
      gsap.fromTo(
        factRows,
        { autoAlpha: 0, y: 12 },
        {
          autoAlpha: 1,
          y: 0,
          stagger: 0.05,
          duration: 0.45,
          ease: MONOGRAPH_EASE,
          scrollTrigger: {
            trigger: '.case-facts',
            start: 'top 90%',
            once: true,
          },
        }
      );
    }

    const nextCard = document.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.case-next__card');
    const nextMedia = nextCard?.querySelector<HTMLElement>('.case-next__media');
    if (nextCard && nextMedia) {
      gsap.fromTo(
        nextMedia,
        { clipPath: 'inset(8% 0% 8% 0%)' },
        {
          clipPath: 'inset(0% 0% 0% 0%)',
          duration: 0.5,
          ease: MONOGRAPH_EASE,
          scrollTrigger: {
            trigger: nextCard,
            start: 'top 92%',
            once: true,
          },
        }
      );
    }
  }, document);

  let cleaned = false;
  const teardown = () => {
    if (cleaned) return;
    cleaned = true;
    controller.abort();
    ctx.revert();
  };

  document.addEventListener('astro:before-swap', teardown, { once: true, signal });

  return teardown;
}

    props: { commission, nextCommission: commissions[(index + 1) % commissions.length] },
  }));
